# PART 1 — Your unit section(s) of FINAL_OUTLINE_v2 (verbatim)

## Appendix O — 光锥排序微扰论与 Drell–Yan 的 Glauber 相消 / Light-cone-ordered perturbation theory and the Glauber cancellation in Drell–Yan

`\label{app:appO}` · `appO_glauber.tex` · **32 pp** · appendix (proofs) · written after the App N labels of §N.3–§N.5 are frozen

**Purpose.** Replace "the Glauber region cancels after the sum over cuts" by a conditional theorem whose every step is either proved or a numbered assumption, for colour-singlet inclusive Drell–Yan at fixed order. The proof follows the hybrid light-cone ordering of Aybat–Sterman (`AybatSterman2009`: $x^+$-ordering for one jet, $x^-$-ordering for the other; the pinch becomes a mismatch of deformation directions; final-state phases cancel by unitarity) and the light-cone treatment of Diehl–Gaunt–Ostermeier–Plößl–Schäfer (`Diehl:2015bca`), on the skeleton of CSS and Bodwin (`CollinsSoperSterman1985b`, `CollinsSoperSterman1988`, `Bodwin1985`, `Collins2011` §14.3–§14.4). Steps: (O-1) regions, domain and windows; (O-2) leading-power form in LCOPT; (O-3) cut sum and one-sided rewriting (unitarity, equal-light-cone-time localization, entire carrier); (O-4) consistency of the subtraction (chain) terms with the rewriting; (O-5) simultaneous deformation out of the Glauber region; (O-6) eikonal approximation and past-pointing Wilson lines; (O-7) soft factor $=1$ and collinear factors $=$ DIS PDFs; (O-8) interface with App N (`cor:appO-interface` → `prop:appN-dy`). Beyond the existing treatments it proves the LCOPT representation at finite $\eta_0$, the cut-position identity with insertions, the equal-time localization with its contact term, the joint localization with ordering weights (replacing an internally inconsistent fixed-order assumption), the deformation target for the class $\mathcal G_0$ and graded configurations with explicit constants, and the eikonal bounds on the deformed contour; it shows that the non-abelian "strict-window residual" is a truncation artefact (exact eikonal box and crossed box, §O.8); it removes the window-edge terms by side-independent windows; and, in a global two-time model, it proves the cancellation of the **uncut-line** side sums of the coincident $O(g^4)$ spectator patterns with explicit estimates (model theorems of §O.5; their physical reading rests on the matching assumption `asm:appO-collinear-ends`, and the cut direct lines remain open).

### O.1 `sec:appO-overview` 架构 (2 pp)

- The problem in one picture (`prop:dy-glauber-pinch`, Ch12): a soft momentum exchanged between the spectators of $A$ and $B$ is trapped in $k^-$ by an initial-state and a final-state denominator of $J_A$ and in $k^+$ by $J_B$; in a single cut graph the contour cannot leave the Glauber region, where the soft approximation fails. A general proof must say after which sums, in which sense (pointwise, integral norm, weak), on which configurations and to which accuracy the cancellation holds.
- **定义 `def:appO-leading-data` — 领头区域的数据** (no tag). $\Gamma$ a DY forward graph for $A(P_A)+B(P_B)\to\gamma^*(q)+X$ (amplitude and conjugate glued along the final state); $\Gamma_C$ the cut graph of the cut $C$; $R$ a leading region (`thm:ir-leading-regions`, `prop:dy-regions`) with the unobserved final jets merged into $H$. (a) *Subgraphs:* $H$ (amplitude side) and $H^\dagger$ (conjugate side); $J_A$, $J_B$, $S$ span the cut; $J_A$ connects to $H$ by one physical parton and any number of longitudinally polarized gluons on each side (likewise $J_B$); soft lines do not attach to $H$, $H^\dagger$ at leading power. (b) *Hard vertex:* for $J_A$ the subgraph $H$ ($H^\dagger$) acts as one vertex $V_H$ ($V_H^\dagger$) (justified in `lem:appO-hard-locality`). (c) *Active and spectator lines:* lines of $J_A$ with an endpoint on $V_H$ or $V_H^\dagger$ are active, all others spectators; total active momenta $l_A$, $l'_A$ ($l_B$, $l'_B$). (d) *Soft momenta and sides:* connection momenta $k_1,\dots,k_s$ oriented **into** the jet they attach to; a direct $J_A$–$J_B$ line is counted once, with $k_j$ into $J_A$ ($-k_j$ into $J_B$); $\sigma_j=+1$ ($-1$) if the attachment vertex is on the amplitude (conjugate) side; an uncut soft line lies on one side. (e) *Class* $\mathcal G_0$: $(\Gamma,R)\in\mathcal G_0$ iff the soft subgraph consists only of direct $J_A$–$J_B$ lines (any number, possibly cut, any colour structure); lines with both ends on one jet are not direct. External hadrons are colour singlets (split into partons by colour-singlet point vertices); coloured external partons are excluded (`rem:dy-dft-premises`). The DY kinematics are those of `def:dy-kin` (Ch12, pointer; the definition restates what it needs, so that the Ch9 statements built on App O do not depend on Ch12). `\uses{def:ir-regions, def:method-regions}`.
- The steps (O-1)–(O-8) (names binding, WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.4 item 4) with their outputs and error types: (O-1) `def:appO-leading-data`, `def:appO-windows`, `def:appO-weak-equivalence` — no error; (O-2) `lem:appO-glauber-pc` (E-S/E-P), `lem:appO-lcopt`, `lem:appO-numerators`, `lem:appO-hard-locality`, `lem:dy-poles-orientation` — exact under `asm:appO-lcopt`; (O-3) `lem:appO-unitarity` (exact), `lem:appO-equal-time`, `lem:appO-joint-localization`, `prop:appO-ss-localize` (E-F), `prop:appO-one-sided` (E-W, via `asm:appO-envelope`); (O-4) chain-term consistency inside `prop:appO-one-sided` (needs `asm:appN-separation`(ii)); (O-5) `lem:appO-no-crossing`, `prop:appO-target` (exact geometry), `asm:appO-deformation`; (O-6) `prop:appO-eikonal` (E-P on the deformed contour), `prop:appO-past-wilson` ([A]); (O-7) `lem:method-soft-unitarity`; (O-8) `cor:appO-interface`. Three error types appear (print this): *scaling statements* (E-S; integral forms by `asm:appN-envelope`), *fibre-integrated relative errors* (E-F; the rewriting is not a pointwise approximation: in a window of width $\sim\nu_A$ the original integrand has pinched poles and the rewritten one is smooth; integrated over the rest by `asm:appO-envelope`), and *conditional terms* (coincident connections under `asm:appO-mixing`(c); soft-angle region under `asm:appO-deformation`(c)).
- `fig:appO-flowchart` (FIGURES_INDEX_v2 S14): solid boxes for (O-1)…(O-8) with the labels above; orange dashed boxes for `asm:appO-lcopt`, `asm:appO-envelope`, `asm:appO-mixing`, `asm:appO-deformation`, `asm:appO-rewritten-cs` with arrows to their uses; orange dashed side boxes `asm:appO-adjacent-swap`, `asm:appO-real-jets`, `asm:appO-collinear-ends` attached only to the model-theorem box (§O.5; not premises of `thm:appO-main`); green box App N (`prop:appN-dy`); margin labels 「$J_A$：时间 $x^+$，能量 $l^-$」「$\sum_C$，最大时刻配对」「$k^-\to k^-+i\sigma\kappa_-$，$k^+\to k^+-i\sigma\kappa_+$」.
- Precedents and credit (print): `AybatSterman2009` (hybrid light-cone ordering; "colour representation the same in amplitude and conjugate"); `Diehl:2015bca` (light-cone perturbation theory for single and double DY; clarifications of the single-DY Glauber argument); `CollinsSoperSterman1985b`, `CollinsSoperSterman1988`, `Bodwin1985` (all-order skeleton); `Collins2011` §14.4.8 (remaining issues). LCOPT rules: `Kogut:1969xa`, `Chang:1973qi`, `Brodsky:1997de`; equivalence with covariant PT: `Ligterink:1994tm`; light-front vacuum: `Collins:2018aqt`; largest-time equation and cutting: `Veltman1963`, `Cutkosky1960`, `tHooftVeltman1974`.

### O.2 `sec:appO-lcopt` 光锥序微扰论 (4 pp)

- **定义 `def:appO-lcopt` — 光锥时间、中间态与能量分母** (no tag). $J$ a connected subgraph (vertex set $V$, $N=|V|\ge2$, internal lines $L$, no self-loops), all plus and transverse components fixed, $l^+\ne0$ on every line. (i) For $J_A$: time $\tau_v\equiv x_v^+$, line energy $l^-$, on-shell energy $E(l)\equiv(\mathbf l_T^2+m_l^2-i0)/(2l^+)$, so $l^2-m_l^2+i0=2l^+(l^--E(l))$ (row 9; $E(l)$ in the lower half-plane for $l^+>0$); for $J_B$ swap $+\leftrightarrow-$. (ii) Each internal line is oriented along $l^+>0$ (antiparticle reading for fermions), tail $t(l)$, head $h(l)$; $q_v^-$ is the minus momentum flowing into $J$ at $v$. (iii) A *compatible ordering* is a bijection $\pi:\{1,\dots,N\}\to V$ with $\pi^{-1}(t(l))<\pi^{-1}(h(l))$; ${\rm Ord}(J)$ their set; the $i$-th intermediate state $S_i^\pi=\{l:\pi^{-1}(t(l))\le i<\pi^{-1}(h(l))\}$ (never empty, $J$ connected), injected energy $Q_i^\pi=\sum_{j\le i}q^-_{\pi(j)}$, denominator $D_i^\pi=Q_i^\pi-\sum_{l\in S_i^\pi}E(l)$. Fourier identity (print): $\int\frac{dl^-}{2\pi}e^{-il^-\tau}\frac{i}{2l^+(l^--E(l))}=\frac{\theta(l^+)\theta(\tau)+\theta(-l^+)\theta(-\tau)}{2|l^+|}e^{-iE(l)\tau}$. `\uses{def:prelim-lightfront}`.
- **引理 `lem:appO-lcopt` — 由 Feynman 积分导出光锥序微扰论（有限 $\eta_0$）** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）Let $J$ be as in `def:appO-lcopt` with numerators $N_l$ independent of internal minus components ("reduced numerators") and vertex factors dropped as constants. For $q^-\in\mathbb R^N$ let $I_J(q^-)=\int\prod_l\frac{dl^-}{2\pi}\prod_v2\pi\delta\big(q_v^-+\sum_{h(l)=v}l^--\sum_{t(l)=v}l^-\big)\prod_l\frac{iN_l}{2l^+(l^--E(l))}$. With $i0\to i\eta_0$, $\eta_0>0$: (a) the integral without the overall $\delta$ converges absolutely; (b) pointwise in $q^-$,
  $I_J(q^-)=2\pi\delta\Big(\sum_vq_v^-\Big)\prod_l\frac{N_l}{2l^+}\sum_{\pi\in{\rm Ord}(J)}\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\frac{i}{D_i^\pi}$;
  (c) the limit $\eta_0\to0^+$ exists in the sense of tempered distributions in $q^-$ (the $N-1$ forms $Q_i^\pi$ are linearly independent). `\uses{def:appO-lcopt, thm:appM-weinberg}`. Proof: full, five steps — (1) single-line time representation $g_l(\Delta)=N_l\theta(\Delta)e^{-iE(l)_{\eta_0}\Delta}/(2l^+)$, $\|g_l\|_{L^1}=|N_l|/\eta_0$; (2) absolute convergence by Weinberg's theorem (`thm:appM-weinberg`): $|L_W|>\dim W$ for every non-zero subspace $W$ of loop flows, since flows in $W$ are cycles of $(V(L_W),L_W)$ and without self-loops $\dim W\le|L_W|-c_W\le|L_W|-1$; (3) time representation $I_J=\int\prod_vd\tau_ve^{-i\sum_vq_v^-\tau_v}\prod_lg_l(\tau_{h(l)}-\tau_{t(l)})$ (Gaussian damping, Fubini, zero circulation); (4) sector decomposition and summation by parts, $\int_0^\infty d\Delta_i\,e^{i\Delta_iD_i^\pi}=i/D_i^\pi$ (${\rm Im}\,D_i^\pi=\sum_{S_i}\eta_0/(2l^+)>0$); (5) distribution limit. Literature: `Kogut:1969xa`, `Chang:1973qi`, `Brodsky:1997de` (rules), `Ligterink:1994tm` (equivalence; minus integrals). Check: `refdoc/work_R7/check_lcopt.py` (8 graphs at one and two loops, agreement $\le10^{-30}$; a directed 3-cycle with ${\rm Ord}=\varnothing$ gives 0). Error type: exact.
- **引理 `lem:appO-numerators` — 分子、瞬时项与瞬时圈** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）Feynman gauge. (i) Fermions: with $l_{\rm on}=(l^+,E(l),\mathbf l_T)$, $\frac{i(\slashed l+m)}{l^2-m^2+i0}=\frac{i(\slashed l_{\rm on}+m)}{2l^+(l^--E(l))}+\frac{i\gamma^+}{2l^+}$; the second term (instantaneous fermion) places both endpoints at equal light-cone time. (ii) Gluon and ghost numerators are $l^-$ independent. (iii) In general $\mathcal N\prod_l\frac{i}{l^2-m_l^2+i0}=\sum_{T\subseteq L}c_T\prod_{l\notin T}\frac{i}{l^2-m_l^2+i0}$ with $c_T$ polynomial in the $q_v^-$ and in minus components of loops inside $T$. (iv) If $T$ is a forest and $J/T$ has no self-loops, the term is the LCOPT sum of `lem:appO-lcopt` for $J/T$ (contact vertices at one time). (v) Otherwise the only non-absolutely-convergent pieces are one-dimensional integrals $\int dx\,x^r$ and $\int dx\,[2l^+(x-E(l))]^{-1}$ ("instantaneous loops"), independent of all external minus energies; hence for **any** linear assignment of their values every term depends on the external minus energies as (polynomial) × (product of LCOPT denominators). `\uses{lem:appO-lcopt}`. Proof: full (substitute $l^-=E(l)+\Delta_l/(2l^+)$, cancel propagators, iterate; private loop variables for $T$-cycles and self-loops). Check: explicit Dirac matrices (`refdoc/work_R7/check_cutsum.py`). Remark to print: light-cone gauge would add gluon instantaneous terms; Feynman gauge is used throughout.
- **注 `rem:appO-contact` — 与 $V_H$ 重合的接触顶点在领头幂次不出现** (no tag). Contracting an instantaneous fermion line between a soft vertex on an active line and $V_H$ produces a contact vertex at $\tau=0$; at leading power it is absent because the leading soft/Glauber coupling to $A$-collinear fermions is $\gamma^+$ and $\gamma^+\gamma^+=0$, and a soft gluon effectively attached to $H$ costs $\lambda^2$. (E-S.) Longitudinal collinear gluons attached to $H$ ($J^+H^-$) are ordinary active lines.
- **假设 `asm:appO-lcopt` — 光锥序微扰论的适用性** [\tagA]. Full text in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. Content: for a DY leading region and $J=J_A$ (likewise $J_B$): (i) (zero modes, endpoints, truncation) on $U_R$, in the integral norm, the regularized Feynman integral of $J$ equals up to $O(\lambda^a)$ the iterated integral "all internal minus components over $\mathbb R$ first (`lem:appO-lcopt`, `lem:appO-numerators`), then plus and transverse components over $\{l^+\ne0\}$"; distributions concentrated at $l^+=0$ and endpoint contributions of plus integrals are absent at leading power, and the minus-component restriction of `def:method-neighbourhoods` can be dropped (removed tail $O(\lambda_J/\lambda^\beta)$); (ii) (instantaneous loops) there is an assignment of the one-dimensional integrals of `lem:appO-numerators`(v) (depending only on plus and transverse components) for which the minus integral equals the expansion of `lem:appO-numerators`. Item (iii) of the earlier formulation (Hermiticity of the cut rules for ghost and three-gluon vertices) is **not** an assumption: it is `lem:appJ-cut-hermiticity` (App J). `\uses{lem:appO-lcopt, lem:appO-numerators, def:method-neighbourhoods}`.
- Figure `fig:appO-lcopt-term` (FIGURES_INDEX_v2 S15): one LCOPT term of $J_A$ (amplitude side) with $V_H$ at $\tau=0$, two soft injections, denominators $D_1,\dots,D_5$ marked initial/final.

### O.3 `sec:appO-orientation` 取向与幂次计数 (3 pp)

- **定义 `def:appO-orientation` — 初态与末态中间态、取向与标准流** (no tag). In an LCOPT term of $J_A$ whose amplitude part contains $V_H$: (a) amplitude-side intermediate states before $V_H$ ($\tau<0$) are *initial*, those after $V_H$ (before the cut) *final*; the conjugate side is mirrored. (b) $k_j$ has orientation $\eta=-1$ ($+1$) in a term if $k_j^-$ appears there in an initial (final) denominator; "$k_j$ flows only through $\eta=-1$ states" means $k_j^-$ appears only in initial denominators in every term (never in final denominators or in the cut $\delta$). Orientation is a property of intermediate states, **not of Feynman lines** (a spectator line can belong to initial and final states). (c) *Standard flow:* route the loop momenta of $J_A$ so that each soft $k_j$ attached at $v_j$ on side $\sigma_j$ enters at $v_j$, flows along a same-side path to $V_H$ ($\sigma_j=+1$) or $V_H^\dagger$ ($\sigma_j=-1$) and exits through the active lines: $l_A=\hat l_A+\sum_{j\in\mathcal A,\sigma_j=+1}k_j$, $l'_A=\hat l'_A+\sum_{j\in\mathcal A,\sigma_j=-1}k_j$, $\hat l_A=\hat l'_A=P_A-\sum_{c\in C\cap J_A}p_c$ (cut momenta soft independent); for $J_B$ use incoming connection momenta. `\uses{def:appO-lcopt, def:appO-leading-data}`.
- **引理 `lem:appO-orientation` — 光锥序中间态的取向与 $k^-$ 极点（精确形式）** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）In the finite-$\eta_0$ LCOPT representation (`lem:appO-lcopt`) of a jet subgraph $J_A$ whose active momenta exit at one vertex $V_H$, with standard flow: (a) every amplitude-side initial denominator has the form $D=A_D+\sum_{j\in I(D)}k_j^-+i0$ and every final one $D=A'_D-\sum_{j\in F(D)}k_j^-+i0$, with $A_D$, $A'_D$ independent of all soft minus components ($I(D)$, $F(D)$: soft momenta injected before, resp. after the state but before the cut); otherwise $k_j^-$ occurs only polynomially in vertex/contact factors, never in $1/(2l^+)$, on-shell numerators or the cut $\delta$; (b) as functions of $k_j^-$, initial zeros lie at ${\rm Im}\,k_j^-<0$ and final zeros at ${\rm Im}\,k_j^->0$; for any $\kappa_j\ge0$ the simultaneous shift $k_j^-\to k_j^-+i\kappa_j$ gives every initial denominator ${\rm Im}\,D=\sum_{I(D)}\kappa_j+\eta_0>0$; conjugate side: $k_j^-\to k_j^--i\kappa_j$; both sides $k^-\to k^-+i\sigma\kappa$; (c) $J_B$: $+\leftrightarrow-$, injected momenta $-k_j$ for direct lines, compatible shift $k_j^+\to k_j^+-i\sigma_j\kappa_j$. Hence the $k^-$ poles of the lines coupling to $k$ lie on one side iff all intermediate states it traverses have one orientation; it generalizes `lem:coll-final-poles` and `prop:dy-glauber-pinch` (pointers). `\uses{def:appO-lcopt, lem:appO-lcopt, lem:appO-numerators, def:appO-orientation}`. Ch12 `lem:dy-poles-orientation` is the chapter-level statement of this lemma and cites it (integrator decision: App O owns the precise lemma, so that the App O results used by the Ch9 statements do not depend on Ch12). *Proof:* full — $Q_i^\pi$ before $V_H$ contains $P_A^-$ plus the soft energies injected so far; after $V_H$ use overall minus conservation of the amplitude part ($P_A^-+K_{\rm tot}-u^-=\mathcal E$, $u$ the active momentum, $\mathcal E=\sum_{\rm cut}E(p_c)$) to write $Q_i^\pi=\mathcal E-K_{>i}$; ${\rm Im}$ of $-E(l)$ is $+\eta_0/(2l^+)$. Orientation conventions vs `lem:method-i0-compat`: there $k_D=-k_j$ flows out of the jet and $\eta_D=-1$ ⇔ initial; the deformations coincide (print the short dictionary: $\eta=-1$ ≡ initial ≡ amplitude-side ${\rm Im}\,k_j^->0$, ${\rm Im}\,k_j^+<0$ for direct lines). Example: `ex:dy-spectator-lcopt` (two orderings = two covariant propagators). `fig:dy-pole-orientation` (owner Ch12).
- **定义 `def:appO-pinch` — Glauber 区与 Glauber 夹挤** (no tag). Fix $0<\delta\le\frac18$. (i) $G_\delta=\{k:\mathbf k_T\ne0,\ |k^+k^-|\le\delta\,\mathbf k_T^2\}$ (moduli of components for complex $k$). (ii) $k_j$ is *pinched in $J_A$* if $k_j^-$ appears in an initial denominator of some term and in a final denominator of the same term (*intra-term*) or another term (*inter-term*); likewise $J_B$ with $k_j^+$. (iii) A direct line is *Glauber-pinched* if pinched in $J_A$ and in $J_B$; for a general soft subgraph a soft loop momentum is Glauber-pinched if one connection momentum it flows through is pinched in $J_A$ and another in $J_B$. Remarks to print: (1) cut lines are never in $G_\delta$ ($2|k^+k^-|=\mathbf k_T^2+m^2>2\delta\mathbf k_T^2$), so Glauber momenta live on uncut lines and $\sigma_j$ is defined; (2) inter-term pinches are obstructions because Ward identities hold only after summing attachments, so all terms must share one contour (the K-term pole at $k^-=0$ lies between up- and down-deformed contours; its residue is the Glauber phase); (3) if $k_j$ is pinched in only one jet, deforming the other component even to $|k_j^+|\sim|\mathbf k_{Tj}|$ does **not** leave $G_\delta$ ($|k^+k^-|\sim\lambda^3\ll\mathbf k_T^2$) — it only validates the soft approximation on that side (misconception M31; `rem:method-two-component`). `\uses{def:ir-glauber, lem:appO-orientation}`.
- **引理 `lem:appO-glauber-pc` — Glauber 幂次计数** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）Scaling region of $R$: $J_A$ lines $\sim(1,\lambda^2,\lambda)$, $J_B$ lines $\sim(\lambda^2,1,\lambda)$, $H$ lines of virtuality $\sim Q^2$, soft $k_j$ soft $(\lambda^2,\lambda^2,\lambda^2)$ or Glauber $(\lambda^2,\lambda^2,\lambda)$ ($Q=1$). (a) In every denominator and numerator of $J_A$ the terms containing $k_j^+$ are relatively $O(\lambda^2)$: $(l+c)^2-m^2=(l^2-m^2)+2l^+c^-+2l^-c^+-2\mathbf l_T\cdot\mathbf c_T+2c^+c^--\mathbf c_T^2$, dropped terms $2l^-c^+$, $2c^+c^-$ ($\sim\lambda^4$ against kept $\sim\lambda^2$); (b) $J_B$ likewise with $k_j^-$; (c) (pointwise) on $G_\delta$ the connecting denominator does not vanish and $\frac{1}{2k^+k^--\mathbf k_T^2+i0}=-\frac{1}{\mathbf k_T^2}(1+r)$, $|r|\le\frac{2\delta}{1-2\delta}$; (d) $H$ and the measurement ($Q^2$, $y$; $\mathbf q_T$ for TMD) depend on $l_A^-$ and on soft minus components routed through $V_H$ only at relative $O(\lambda^2)$, on $l_B^+$ likewise, on transverse components at relative $O(\lambda)$ (TMD: only through $\delta^{(2)}(\mathbf q_T-\dots)$): $H=H(l_A^+,l_B^-;q)$ at leading power; (e) for TMD soft momenta $(\lambda,\lambda,\lambda)$ ($\lambda=q_T/Q$) the ratios of (a)(b)(d) become relative $O(\lambda)$; at the Glauber scale (a)–(d) are unchanged. `\uses{def:appO-leading-data, def:ir-glauber}`. Proof: full (expansion of the denominators; (c) $r=x/(1-x)$, $|x|=|2k^+k^-/\mathbf k_T^2|\le2\delta$). Error type: (a)(b)(d)(e) **E-S** (scaling statements, not pointwise bounds near zeros of jet denominators; integral forms by `asm:appN-envelope`, `rem:ir-scaling-vs-bound`); (c) **E-P**.
- **引理 `lem:appO-hard-locality` — 硬顶点的光锥定域性与无权变量 $\mathcal E$** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appO-lcopt}。（本引理只涉及部分子态。）After the approximation of `lem:appO-glauber-pc`(d) ($H$ and the measurement ignore $J_A$'s active minus components), in the cut graph: (o) the amplitude part of $J_A$ together with $V_H$ is connected (plus-momentum conservation; a component without the hadron would be a closed collinear subgraph attached only to soft lines, not on a pinch surface); (i) integrating the active minus components (unweighted) fixes $V_H$ and $V_H^\dagger$ at $\tau=0$; (ii) after this integration
  $J_A^{\rm cut}=\sum_{\pi,\pi'}\mathcal F_{\pi\pi'}\prod_{i<i_H}\frac{i}{P_A^-+K^\pi_{\le i}-\sum_{S_i^\pi}E(l)}\prod_{i\ge i_H}\frac{i}{\mathcal E-K^\pi_{>i}-\sum_{S_i^\pi}E(l)}\times\big[\text{conjugate}\big]^*$,
  with $\mathcal F_{\pi\pi'}$ independent of all minus components, $\mathcal E=\sum_{p\in X}E(p)$ an unweighted variable fixed by the cut's $2\pi\delta(\mathcal E-\sum_XE(p))$; initial denominators do not depend on $\mathcal E$, final ones not on $P_A^-$; (iii) the standard flow of `def:appO-orientation` exists and is legitimate (different paths differ by exact translations of plus and transverse loop variables and by changes of $H$ and the measurement of relative order $\lambda^2$, resp. $\lambda$); (iv) in operator language the PDF fields sit at $x^+=0$; for TMD the active transverse weight $e^{i\mathbf b\cdot\mathbf l_{TA}}$ contains no minus component. `\uses{asm:appO-lcopt, lem:appO-lcopt, lem:appO-numerators, lem:appO-glauber-pc, def:appO-orientation}`. Proof: full (injections $-u^-$ at $V_H$, $P_A^-$ at the hadron, $k_j^-$ at soft vertices; relative active minus components give $\delta(\tau_r-\tau_{r'})$; $u^-$, $u'^-$ appear only through $\exp[iu^-(\tau_{V_H}-\tau'_{V_H^\dagger})]$). Error type: exact given `asm:appO-lcopt` and the E-S approximation (d). Pointer: `rem:dy-one-time-per-jet` (one light-cone time per jet: ordering the whole graph in one $x^+$ would spread the $B$ end of the hard vertex over $O(1/Q)$ in $x^+$, exactly the Glauber range).

### O.4 `sec:appO-unitarity` 割图求和的幺正性 (3 pp)

- **定义 `def:appO-chain` — 末态链、割位置与约化权重** (no tag). Fix the initial orderings on both sides. List the final-part vertices of $J_A$ in closed-time-path order (amplitude $\tau>0$ vertices increasing in $\tau$, then conjugate $\tau>0$ vertices decreasing): $v_1,\dots,v_n$ with states $s_0,\dots,s_n$ ($s_0$ right after $V_H$, $s_n$ right before $V_H^\dagger$); the cut crosses $s_j$ ($0\le j\le n$). Reduced weights: amplitude vertex $\alpha_i=(-iv_i)\cdot i$, conjugate vertex $\beta_i=(+iv_i)\cdot(-i)$, with $v_i$ the numerical vertex factor (on-shell numerator × coupling); amplitude insertions (attachment points of soft lines lying on the amplitude side) have $\beta_i=0$, conjugate insertions $\alpha_i=0$. State energies $E_i=\sum_{l\in s_i}E(l)+\sum_{\text{amp. ins. }a>i}k_a^-+\sum_{\text{conj. ins. }b\le i}k_b'^-$ (independent of $j$). The term with the cut through $s_j$: $\mathcal T_j=W_j\int\frac{d\mathcal E}{2\pi}\prod_{i<j}\frac{1}{\mathcal E-E_i+i0}\,2\pi\delta(\mathcal E-E_j)\prod_{i>j}\frac{1}{\mathcal E-E_i-i0}$, $W_j=\prod_{i\le j}\alpha_i\prod_{i>j}\beta_i$. `\uses{def:appO-orientation, def:prelim-cut}`.
- **引理 `lem:appO-cut-position` — 割位置恒等式（含插入）** (数学). $E_0,\dots,E_n$ real; $\alpha_i,\beta_i\in\mathbb C$ independent of $\mathcal E$; $\pm i0\to\pm i\eta_0$, $2\pi\delta(x)\to2\eta_0/(x^2+\eta_0^2)$. With $H_m(\mathcal E)=\prod_{i<m}\frac{1}{\mathcal E-E_i+i0}\prod_{i\ge m}\frac{1}{\mathcal E-E_i-i0}$ ($1\le m\le n$):
  $\sum_{j=0}^n\mathcal T_j=\delta_{n0}W_0-i\sum_{m=1}^n(\alpha_m-\beta_m)\prod_{i<m}\alpha_i\prod_{i>m}\beta_i\int\frac{d\mathcal E}{2\pi}H_m(\mathcal E)$,
  exactly for every $\eta_0>0$. (a) If $\alpha_i=\beta_i$ for all $i$, $\sum_j\mathcal T_j=\delta_{n0}$. (b) Only vertices with $\alpha_m\ne\beta_m$ contribute; an amplitude insertion contributes only if it is the last amplitude insertion with no conjugate insertion before it, a conjugate insertion only if it is the first conjugate insertion with no amplitude insertion after it: at most two terms. `\uses{def:appO-chain}`. Proof: full (with $a_i=(\mathcal E-E_i+i\eta_0)^{-1}$, $b_i=(\mathcal E-E_i-i\eta_0)^{-1}$: $\prod_{i<j}a_i\,i(a_j-b_j)\prod_{i>j}b_i=i(H_{j+1}-H_j)$; Abel summation; $\int H_0=\int H_{n+1}=0$ for $n\ge1$). Interpretation (print, Veltman's largest-time equation in light-cone time, `Veltman1963`): moving the cut across the latest vertex changes only its weight. Check: `refdoc/work_R7/check_cutsum.py` ($n=0..6$, random complex weights, agreement $\le10^{-39}$; insertion patterns ICA, CIA, AICIA give 0). Generalizes the $\alpha=\beta$ telescoping of c_glauber §1.5 Step 5.
- **引理 `lem:appO-unitarity` — 光锥幺正性引理（闭时路径形式）** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appO-lcopt}。（本引理只涉及部分子态。）Under the approximations of `lem:appO-glauber-pc`(a)(d), fix everything outside $J_A$: amplitude insertions $O_a$ (the good component $J^{+a}$ of the colour current at a soft attachment, integrated over $x^-$, Fourier transformed in $\mathbf x_T$), conjugate insertions $O'_b$, and their external factors; sum over all measurement-compatible cuts of $J_A$ (keeping every insertion on its side) and integrate $\mathcal E$ unweighted. Then (a) $\mathcal J_A=\int\prod_ad\tau_ae^{-ik_a^-\tau_a}\prod_bd\tau'_be^{+ik_b'^-\tau'_b}\langle P_A|\bar{\rm T}\{V_H^\dagger(0)\prod_bO_b'^\dagger(\tau'_b)\}\,{\rm T}\{V_H(0)\prod_aO_a(\tau_a)\}|P_A\rangle$, and the terms of a given forward graph equal the sum of its side-preserving cuts; (b) in each chain, contributions of internal vertices later than all insertions cancel in the cut sum; without $\tau>0$ insertions $\mathcal J_A=\langle\Psi'|\Psi\rangle$ with $|\Psi\rangle=V_H(0){\rm T}\{\prod_aO_a(\tau_a)\}|P_A\rangle$: final-state interactions internal to $J_A$ are absent; (c) two families of cut graphs differing only in the side of one insertion that is the latest vertex sum to $(\alpha_m-\beta_m)$ times a common factor. `\uses{asm:appO-lcopt, lem:appO-hard-locality, lem:appO-cut-position, lem:appJ-cut-hermiticity}`. Proof: full — (b)(c) from `lem:appO-cut-position` with $\alpha_i=\beta_i$ for internal vertices (`lem:appJ-cut-hermiticity`); (a) apply `lem:appO-cut-position` chain by chain ($H_m$ = "$v_m$ latest"), equivalently Gell-Mann–Low with $U^\dagger U=1$ (the light-front vacuum is trivial by `asm:appO-lcopt`(i)). Error type: exact (given the E-S approximations). Gauge: the identity is algebraic and needs no positive metric.
- **注 `rem:appO-cut-sum-content` — 幺正性引理用到了什么、没有消去什么** (no tag). (1) The only physical input is that the identity operator stands between cut states ($J_A$'s final state unconstrained, $\mathcal E$ unweighted) — "compatible with the measurement"; if a final hadron is measured or a gap imposed, the projector does not commute with the evolution and (b)(c) fail (pointer `prop:break-measurement`, Ch18). (2) What does not cancel: soft lines pin insertions to a side, so $\tau>0$ insertions cannot be paired within $J_A$ alone — the source of the Glauber pinch.
- Figure `fig:appO-cutsum` (FIGURES_INDEX_v2 S16): one chain in closed-time-path order with the four cut positions; for a fixed time configuration whose latest vertex is internal the two adjacent cut positions cancel.

### O.5 `sec:appO-localization` 等光锥时刻局域化 (5 pp)

Why equal time (print, one paragraph): in $G_\delta$ the connecting propagator $\simeq-1/\mathbf k_T^2$ is $k^\pm$ independent, so in coordinate space it is $\propto\delta(x^+-y^+)\delta(x^--y^-)$; $J_A$ ignores $k^+$ (attachments at $x^-\simeq0$), $J_B$ ignores $k^-$ (at $y^+\simeq0$): the Glauber exchange happens at the hard vertex's time, when the two contracted hadrons pass through each other.

- **定义 `def:appO-windows` — 可局域端点、尺度适配窗口与软角区** (no tag). $\beta_R$ from `def:method-neighbourhoods`; fix $0<a_G<\min_{R'}\beta_{R'}$. (a) An endpoint $j$ of an uncut soft line in $J_A$ is *localizable* if it sits on a spectator line of $J_A$ or on an active line emitted by $V_H$, and the other end is not in $J_A$; a *localizable unit* is a localizable endpoint or an SS line (direct line with both ends on spectators, one unit). (b) Jet energy scale $\nu_A\equiv P_A^-+\sum_{l\in J_A}E(l)$ (all lines of $J_A$, both sides and cut, $l^+>0$; independent of soft $\pm$ components after `lem:appO-glauber-pc`(a); $\nu_A\sim\lambda^2$ in the scaling region). (c) With $\vartheta\in C^\infty(\mathbb R)$, $0\le\vartheta\le1$, $\vartheta=1$ on $(-\infty,\frac12]$, $\vartheta=0$ on $[1,\infty)$; $\mu_j\equiv\lambda^{\beta_R}$ for an endpoint in $J_A$, $\mu_j\equiv\nu_B$ for an SS line: $\Theta_j\equiv\vartheta\big(\frac{4\nu_A\mu_j}{\lambda^{a_G}\mathbf k_{Tj}^2}\big)$, $\Theta'_j\equiv\vartheta\big(\frac{8\nu_A\mu_j}{\lambda^{a_G}\mathbf k_{Tj}^2}\big)$, carrier width $w_j\equiv\lambda^{a_G}|\mathbf k_{Tj}|$; $J_B$ by $A\leftrightarrow B$; ${\rm supp}\,\Theta'_j\subset\{\Theta_j=1\}$. (d) Soft-angle region $K_R\equiv\{x\in U_R:\prod_u\Theta'_u(x)<1\}$. The windows depend only on soft $\mathbf k_T$ and jet plus/transverse components, never on soft $\pm$ components (they commute with fibre integration and with the deformation of §O.7); Glauber $|\mathbf k_T|\sim\lambda$ lies inside the windows, the genuine soft scale $|\mathbf k_T|\sim\lambda^2$ in $K_R$. (e) For the SS lines of a spectator-exchange family the side-independent windows $\hat\Theta_m$ of `def:appO-family` replace $\Theta_j$ (slot X1c; `lem:appO-side-independent-windows`). Optional figure `fig:appO-windows`. `\uses{def:method-neighbourhoods, def:appO-leading-data}`.
- **引理 `lem:appO-equal-time` — 等光锥时刻插入** (数学). Let $\mathcal J:\mathbb R\to\mathbb C$, $J^{\rm in}(k)=\int_{-\infty}^0d\tau\,e^{-ik\tau}\mathcal J(\tau)$, $J^{\rm fi}(k)=\int_0^\infty d\tau\,e^{-ik\tau}\mathcal J(\tau)$, $J=J^{\rm in}+J^{\rm fi}$. Assume **(ET1)** $J^{\rm in}$ analytic in ${\rm Im}\,k>0$, $J^{\rm fi}$ in ${\rm Im}\,k<0$, both continuous up to the real axis; **(ET2)** constants $\mathcal J_\pm$, $M\ge|\mathcal J_\pm|$, $\lambda_J>0$ with $|J^{\rm in}(k)-\frac{i\mathcal J_-}{k}|\le\frac{M\lambda_J}{|k|^2}$ (${\rm Im}\,k\ge0$, $|k|\ge2\lambda_J$) and $|J^{\rm fi}(k)+\frac{i\mathcal J_+}{k}|\le\frac{M\lambda_J}{|k|^2}$ (${\rm Im}\,k\le0$, $|k|\ge2\lambda_J$); $R$ analytic in $|k|<\Lambda$ with $|R|\le C_R|R(0)|$ there, $\Lambda\ge8\lambda_J$. Then: (i) if $\mathcal J$ is piecewise $C^1$ near 0 with integrable derivative, $\mathcal J_\pm=\mathcal J(0^\pm)$; (ii) (window form) for $2\lambda_J\le K\le\Lambda/2$: $\int_{-K}^K\frac{dk}{2\pi}JR=R(0)\frac{\mathcal J_++\mathcal J_-}{2}+\frac{iKR'(0)}{\pi}(\mathcal J_--\mathcal J_+)+O\big(M|R(0)|[\frac{\lambda_J}{K}+\frac{K^2}{\Lambda^2}]\big)$; for an asymmetric window $[-K_1,K_2]$ with $2\lambda_J\le K_{1,2}\le\Lambda/2$ the same holds with error $O(M|R(0)|[\lambda_J/\min(K_1,K_2)+\max(K_1,K_2)^2/\Lambda^2])$, and if $\mathcal J_+=\mathcal J_-$ the $K^2/\Lambda^2$ term is absent; (iii) (full axis) if moreover $R=\sum_ir_i/(k-z_i)$ is a finite sum of simple poles with $|z_i|\ge2\Lambda$, $R_\downarrow$ ($R_\uparrow$) the terms with ${\rm Im}\,z_i<0$ ($>0$) and $\|R\|=\sum_i|r_i/z_i|$:
  $\int_{-\infty}^\infty\frac{dk}{2\pi}JR=\mathcal J_+R_\downarrow(0)+\mathcal J_-R_\uparrow(0)+O\big(\frac{M\lambda_J}{\Lambda}\|R\|\big)=R(0)\big[\mathcal J_-+c\,(\mathcal J_+-\mathcal J_-)\big]+O\big(\frac{M\lambda_J}{\Lambda}\|R\|\big)$, $c\equiv R_\downarrow(0)/R(0)$.
  `% uses: none`. Proof: full — (i) integration by parts; (ii) move $J^{\rm in}$ to the upper and $J^{\rm fi}$ to the lower semicircle of radius $K$ ($\int_{C^\pm}dk/k=\mp i\pi$), expand $R=R(0)+R'(0)k+\rho$ with Cauchy estimates; asymmetric windows: semicircle of radius $\min K$ plus a real segment where (ET2) holds; (iii) **corrected proof**: $\int J^{\rm in}R_\downarrow=0=\int J^{\rm fi}R_\uparrow$; write $J^{\rm fi}=-i\mathcal J_+/(k-i\lambda_J)+E(k)$ with $E$ analytic in the closed lower half-plane and $|E|\le3M\lambda_J/|k|^2$; close in the lower half-plane: $\int\frac{dk}{2\pi}ER_\downarrow=-i\sum_{{\rm Im}z_i<0}r_iE(z_i)$, $|\cdot|\le\frac{3M\lambda_J}{2\Lambda}\|R\|$ (no derivative bound on the real axis is needed). Check: `refdoc/work_R7/check_e28.py`, `check_e28_asym.py` (error $\propto\lambda_J/\Lambda$; window errors within bound; asymmetric windows $O(\lambda_J/\min K)$). Surpass: ERR-m26 (proof of (iii); window-shape condition).
- **引理 `lem:appO-insertion-values` — 光锥序中的插入：(ET1)(ET2) 与等时值** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）For one insertion $O$ (amplitude side, injected energy $k\equiv k^-$, other variables real) into a leading-power LCOPT jet factor $J_A$ — a sum of ordering terms with $V_H$ at $\tau=0$ and denominators of the form of `lem:dy-poles-orientation`, terms polynomial in $k$ dropped as in `lem:appO-glauber-pc` (the cut-summed $J_A$ of `lem:appO-unitarity` is of this form): (a) (ET1)(ET2) of `lem:appO-equal-time` hold with $\lambda_J\le C_\Gamma(\nu_A+\sum_i|k_i^-|)$ (other not yet localized insertions), $\nu_A$ the jet energy scale of `def:appO-windows`; (b) $\mathcal J_-$ ($\mathcal J_+$) is the sum of the LCOPT terms with $O$ immediately before (after) $V_H$; in operator language ${\rm T}\{V_H(0)O(0^-)\}=V_H(0)O(0)$, ${\rm T}\{V_H(0)O(0^+)\}=O(0)V_H(0)$, so $\mathcal J_+-\mathcal J_-$ is a matrix element of $[O(0),V_H(0)]$ and equals $\mathcal J_+^{\rm act}-\mathcal J_-^{\rm act}$ (terms with $O$ on an active line adjacent to $V_H$); (c) if $O$ sits on a spectator and no other non-commuting insertion is localized at $\tau=0$ on the same side and parton, $\mathcal J_+=\mathcal J_-\equiv\mathcal J(0)$, and $J(k)=O(\lambda_J/k^2)$ for $|k|\gg\lambda_J$: eikonal-region soft gluons do not couple to spectators at leading power (LCOPT form of the decoupling in `lem:method-ward-multi`); (d) every chain term $T_c$ of `def:method-subtraction` preserves (ET1)(ET2) with the same constants (eikonalized lines have zero on-shell energy, so $\lambda_J$ only decreases). `\uses{lem:appO-equal-time, lem:appO-orientation, lem:appO-glauber-pc, def:appO-windows, def:method-subtraction}`. Proof: full (by `lem:dy-poles-orientation`, $J^{\rm in}$ has only initial and $J^{\rm fi}$ only final denominators; $|(A_D+k)^{-1}-k^{-1}|\le2|A_D|/|k|^2$; zero-duration states between $O$ and $V_H$ for spectator lines). Error type: exact given the E-S approximations.
- **注 `rem:appO-constant-c` — 常数 $c$ 与正规化依赖性** (no tag). For a direct line at fixed $k^+\ne0$, $\mathbf k_T$: $R\propto(2k^+k-\mathbf k_T^2+i0)^{-1}$, one pole at $\mathbf k_T^2/(2k^+)$ (lower half-plane for $k^+>0$); with $\Lambda=\mathbf k_T^2/(4|k^+|)$, `lem:appO-equal-time`(iii) gives $c=\theta(k^+)$, whereas the $G_\delta$ fibre window $|k|\le\delta\mathbf k_T^2/|k^+|$ gives $c=\frac12-\frac{2i}{\pi}\delta\,{\rm sgn}(k^+)+O(\delta^3)$ (check: `refdoc/work_R7/check_e28.py`). The two differ by contributions from outside $G_\delta$ (connecting line near shell). Only active-line insertions ($\mathcal J_+\ne\mathcal J_-$) feel the choice: ordering convention ${\rm T}_c\{V_H(0)O(0)\}=V_H(0)O(0)+c[O(0),V_H(0)]$ (contact term). Window errors contain $O(\delta^2)$, not a power of $\lambda$; hence the rewriting uses the full-axis form (iii).
- **命题 `prop:appO-ss-localize` — 一条旁观者–旁观者线的局域化与 Glauber 算符** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appO-lcopt}。（本命题只涉及部分子态。）$(\Gamma,R)\in\mathcal G_0$; one amplitude-side SS line $j$ with the other insertions real and fixed, $\Lambda_j\equiv\mathbf k_{Tj}^2/(4|k_j^+|)$, $G_j=(2k_j^+k_j^--\mathbf k_{Tj}^2+i0)^{-1}$ kept exact (not replaced by $-1/\mathbf k_{Tj}^2$). (i) For $\Lambda_j\ge8\lambda_J^A$: $\big|\int\frac{dk_j^-}{2\pi}J_AR-R(0)\mathcal J_A(0)\big|\le C'M\lambda_J^A|R(0)|/\Lambda_j$ (**absolute** bound; $\mathcal J_A(0)$ may be small relative to $M$); (ii) after both fibre integrals ($k_j^-$, then $k_j^+$, using $\int\frac{dk^+}{2\pi}J_B=\mathcal J_B(0)$ and the tail $J_B=O(\nu_B/(k^+)^2)$) the error is bounded by $C\,M|R(0)|\,x(1+\ln\frac1x)$, $x\equiv\nu_A\nu_B/\mathbf k_{Tj}^2$, which is $O(\lambda^{a_G}L)$ on the SS window ($4\nu_A\nu_B\le\lambda^{a_G}\mathbf k_T^2$); the logarithm is genuine; (iii) in the window the line inserts, in the closed-time-path matrix element of $J_A\otimes J_B$ at $\tau_A=\tau_B=0$, the operator $-iV_G$ (conjugate side $+iV_G$), $V_G=\int d^2\mathbf b\,d^2\mathbf b'\,\rho_A^a(\mathbf b)\chi(\mathbf b-\mathbf b')\rho_B^a(\mathbf b')$, $\chi(\mathbf b)=g^2\int\frac{d^2\mathbf k_T}{(2\pi)^2}\frac{e^{i\mathbf k_T\cdot\mathbf b}}{\mathbf k_T^2}\Theta(\mathbf k_T)$, $\rho_A^a(\mathbf b)=\int dx^-J_A^{+a}(0,x^-,\mathbf b)$ restricted to spectators, coupling $+igJ^{\mu a}A^a_\mu$ (binding convention); on the window core ($\Theta=1$ for all side assignments) $\chi$ is a real even kernel in $\mathbf k_T$ alone and $V_G$ is Hermitian and diagonal in transverse positions; $\Theta$ cuts off $\mathbf k_T\to0$, so $\chi$ is IR finite. `\uses{asm:appO-lcopt, lem:appO-unitarity, lem:appO-equal-time, lem:appO-insertion-values, lem:appO-glauber-pc, def:appO-windows}`. Proof: full for (i)(iii); (ii) full for $|k_j^+|\le X_j\equiv\mathbf k_T^2/(32C_\Gamma\nu_A)$, scaling estimate beyond (E-S). Check: `refdoc/work_R7/check_e31.py` (relative error $/[x(1+\ln(1/x))]\approx0.41$ constant over eight decades). Normalization: $(+ig)^2(-i)/(-\mathbf k_T^2)$ per exchange; agrees with $\chi=-\frac{e_Ae_B}{2\pi}K_0(m_gb)$ of `prop:dy-abelian-model` after $e_Ae_B\to g^2\mathbf T_A\cdot\mathbf T_B$ and with the Rothstein–Stewart phase ($e^{-iV_G}=e^{i\phi}$, `RothsteinStewart2016`). Error type: E-F (plus E-S beyond $X_j$). Surpass: ERR-m26 (vertex $+ig$; absolute bound).
- **定义 `def:appO-sectors` — 插入扇区、类 (SEC) 与结构条件 (EQ)(ADJ)(REAL)** (no tag). $N$ amplitude-side insertions into a jet $J$ with injected light-cone energies $q=(q_1,\dots,q_N)$ ($q=k^-$ for $J_A$; $q=p\equiv-k^+$ for $J_B$). (a) A *sector* $\sigma$ splits $\{1,\dots,N\}$ into the insertions before $V_H$, in time order $b_1,\dots,b_n$ (set $B_\sigma$), and those after, $f_1,\dots,f_m$ (set $F_\sigma$); gap momenta $K^B_l=\sum_{l'\le l}q_{b_{l'}}$, $K^F_l=\sum_{l'\ge l}q_{f_{l'}}$; colour order $b_1\cdots b_nf_1\cdots f_m$. (b) **(SEC)**: $J=\sum_\sigma J^{(\sigma)}$ with $J^{(\sigma)}(q)=\sum_tc_t\prod_l\phi^t_{B,l}(K^B_l)\prod_l\phi^t_{F,l}(K^F_l)$ (finite sums), each initial gap function a product of $n^t_l\ge1$ factors $i/(A+K+i0)$, each final one of $n^t_l\ge1$ factors $i/(A'-K+i0)$, energies real with $\mu_J\le|A|,|A'|\le\lambda_J$; equal-time value $\mathcal J_\sigma=\sum_{t:\ {\rm all}\ n^t_l=1}c_t$; norms $M_\sigma=\sum_t|c_t|\lambda_J^{-d_t}$, $M^\flat_\sigma=\sum_t|c_t|\mu_J^{-d_t}$ ($d_t=\sum_l(n^t_l-1)$); eikonal product $e_\sigma=\prod_l(-iK^B_l)^{-1}\prod_l(iK^F_l)^{-1}$. (SEC) is the LCOPT form of a leading-power cut-summed jet factor with insertions; it follows from `lem:appO-lcopt`, `lem:appO-numerators`, `lem:appO-orientation` under `asm:appO-lcopt`. (c) **(EQ)**: for insertions on spectators all $\mathcal J_\sigma$ are equal (colour stripped) — verified in LCOPT (`lem:appO-insertion-values`(b),(c)); **(ADJ)**: two sectors of one jet that differ only by the order of two adjacent operations among (insertion 1, insertion 2, $V_H$) have their terms with a single-state gap between the two operations in bijection, with equal coefficients and equal other gap functions (on all-single-state terms (ADJ) implies (EQ)); **(REAL)**: every colour-stripped jet function is a fixed phase times a rational function with real coefficients. (ADJ) and (REAL) hold exactly in the scalar spectator model (`ex:dy-spectator-lcopt`) and are argued, not proven, for spin-summed leading-power LCOPT jets; applied to QCD jets they are the entries `asm:appO-adjacent-swap`, `asm:appO-real-jets`. The names (SEC), (EQ), (ADJ), (REAL) are local to App O. `\uses{def:appO-lcopt, def:appO-orientation}`.
- **引理 `lem:appO-joint-localization` — 非对易插入的联合局域化：扇区规则、次序权重与误差界** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）$J$ satisfies (SEC) (`def:appO-sectors`) with $N$ insertions on one spectator parton and side; external factors $R_e(k)=r_e/(k-z_e)$ with ${\rm Im}\,z_e\ne0$, $\mathcal F=\{e:{\rm Im}\,z_e<0\}$, $\mathcal B=\{e:{\rm Im}\,z_e>0\}$ (direct lines: $z_e=\mathbf k_{Te}^2/(2k_e^+)\mp i0$, the $J_B$ dependence frozen); every $i0$ of $J$ replaced by $i\eta$, $\eta>0$; the statements hold for each $\eta$ and as $\eta\to0^+$. (a) *(exact sector rule)* the integral converges absolutely and $\int\prod_e\frac{dk_e}{2\pi}J\prod_eR_e=[\prod_{e\in\mathcal F}(-ir_e)\prod_{e\in\mathcal B}(ir_e)]\sum_{\sigma:\,B_\sigma=\mathcal B}J^{(\sigma)}(z_1,\dots,z_N)$: insertions with poles in the upper half-plane ($k_e^+<0$) precede $V_H$, those in the lower half-plane follow it. (b) *(error bound)* if $\kappa\equiv\min|K_l(z)|\ge2\lambda_J$ over the contributing sectors and their gaps, $|J^{(\sigma)}(z)-\mathcal J_\sigma e_\sigma(z)|\le N2^{N+1}M_\sigma\frac{\lambda_J}\kappa\prod_l|K_l(z)|^{-1}$, hence $|\int\cdots-\prod_eR_e(0)\sum_\sigma w_\sigma\mathcal J_\sigma|\le|\prod_eR_e(0)|\,N2^{N+1}\frac{\lambda_J}\kappa\sum_\sigma M_\sigma|w_\sigma|$ with $w_\sigma=\frac{\prod_{e\in\mathcal F}z_e}{\prod_lK^F_l(z)}\frac{\prod_{e\in\mathcal B}z_e}{\prod_lK^B_l(z)}$ — the factor $\sum_\sigma|w_\sigma|$ is needed for general complex poles; *closed-quadrant lemma*: if the poles of each group lie in one closed cone of opening $\pi/2$, then $|w_\sigma|\le1$ and $\sum_\sigma|w_\sigma|\le n_F!\,n_B!\le N!$; *Glauber case*: within each group the $z_e$ have one sign, $w_\sigma\in[0,1]$, $\sum_\sigma w_\sigma=1$, and under (EQ) the relative error is $O(m\,\lambda_J|k^+|/\mathbf k_T^2)$. (c) *(weights)* $\sum_\sigma w_\sigma=1$ (eikonal identity); for real poles of one sign in each group $w_\sigma$ is the probability of an exponential race with rates $|z_e|$: in the final group (all $k_e^+>0$) $w_\pi=\prod_jz_j\big/\prod_m\sum_{l\ge m}z_{\pi(l)}$ ($N=2$: $w_{12}=z_1/(z_1+z_2)$); the initial group (all $k_e^+<0$) is **time-reversed**, $w_\pi=\prod_jz_j\big/\prod_m\sum_{l\le m}z_{\pi(l)}$; for mixed signs the weight of a sector is the **product** of the two groups' race weights. (d) *(as functions of $k^+$)* the colour-order weights $W_\pi(k^+)=\sum_{\sigma\ {\rm of\ order}\ \pi}w_\sigma$ are homogeneous of degree 0 and rational in each orthant, **continuous** wherever at most one $k_e^+$ vanishes (as $k_e^+\to0^\pm$ the insertion moves next to $V_H$ from either side — the same colour position), but **not analytic** there (a kink: for $N=2$, $k_2^+>0$, $W_{(12)}=1-a_2k_1^+/(a_1k_2^+)+\dots$ as $k_1^+\to0^+$ and $W_{(12)}\equiv1$ for $k_1^+<0$, $a_e=\mathbf k_{Te}^2$), and discontinuous where two or more components vanish; for an insertion on an active line emitted by $V_H$ the weight is $\theta(k_e^+)$. (e) *(strict window)* with the strict approximation $G_j\to-1/\mathbf k_{Tj}^2$ and independent symmetric truncations in $k^-$ and $k^+$, each jet's order appears with weight $\frac12$ independently (for $N=2$: $w=\frac14$ for each of the four ordering pairs; general shapes: `prop:appO-strict-window-shape`). (f) *(rotated contour)* for $N=2$ with the poles of each group on one ray of the imaginary axis, $|J^{(\sigma)}(z)-\mathcal J_\sigma e_\sigma|\le2M_\sigma(\lambda_J/\kappa)\prod_l|K_l|^{-1}$ whenever $\kappa\ge\lambda_J$. `\uses{def:appO-sectors, lem:appO-equal-time, lem:appO-insertion-values}`. Proof: full — (a) close each $k_e$ contour on the side free of sector-compatible poles (final variables in the lower, initial ones in the upper half-plane; only $R_e$ contributes); (b) telescoping over single-state gaps and the bound $|c_t|\lambda_J^{-d_t}2^{N+1}(\lambda_J/\kappa)$ for multi-state terms; (c) the race integrals partition $\mathbb R^n_+$ (a rational identity, extended to complex $z$); (d) from (c); (e) symmetric window: the principal value vanishes and each $\delta$ part gives $\frac12$; (f) $|A+K|\ge\max(|A|,|K|)$ for imaginary $K$. Checks: X1 scripts `checks_misc.py`, `checks_C2_C3_C4.py`, `fix_r2_scripts/l1_bound_quadrant.py` (the uncorrected bound fails on an explicit $N=2$ example with complex poles; the corrected one holds), `refdoc/work_R7/check_weights_colour.py`; VF-REF-12. Error type: (a)(c)(d)(e)(f) exact, (b) E-F. **Replaces** the fixed-order hypothesis (c1) of the earlier formulation, false per graph (a fixed order differs from the true value by $w_{21}[O_1,O_2]=O(1)$; ERR-S3): the rewritten $J_A$ depends on $k_j^+$ through the weights — continuously, but non-analytically at $k_j^+=0$ —, the obstruction shared with H-emission endpoints, so coincident SA/AS insertions join `asm:appO-deformation`(d). Known: the race formula and $\sum w=1$ (the eikonal identity; the sector-sum identity of `AybatSterman2009` eq. (10)); ours: the half-plane sector rule, exactness at finite $\eta$, the bound with $\sum_\sigma|w_\sigma|$, the closed-quadrant lemma and the $k^+$ dependence — a sharpened result, not marked new (the initial-group and mixed-sign forms correct the earlier planning text, claim N13).
- **引理 `lem:appO-multipole-localization` — 多极点外部因子的联合局域化** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）$J$ satisfies (SEC); each $R_e$ is a proper rational function of $k_e$ with simple poles $p\in P_e$, ${\rm Im}\,p\ne0$, residues $\rho_{e,p}$ (higher-order poles as limits); $s(p)=-i$ for ${\rm Im}\,p<0$, $+i$ for ${\rm Im}\,p>0$. (a) $\int\prod_e\frac{dk_e}{2\pi}J\prod_eR_e=\sum_{(p_e)\in\prod_eP_e}[\prod_es(p_e)\rho_{e,p_e}]\sum_{\sigma:\,B_\sigma=\{e:{\rm Im}\,p_e>0\}}J^{(\sigma)}(p_1,\dots,p_N)$. (b) If $|K_l(p)|\ge\kappa\ge2\lambda_J$ for every pole choice and contributing sector, the integral equals $\sum_{(p)}\prod_e(-\rho_{e,p_e}/p_e)\sum_\sigma w_\sigma(p)\mathcal J_\sigma+{\rm Err}$ with $|{\rm Err}|\le N2^{N+1}\frac{\lambda_J}\kappa\sum_{(p)}\prod_e|\rho_{e,p_e}/p_e|\sum_\sigma M_\sigma|w_\sigma(p)|$; under the closed-quadrant condition for every pole choice $|{\rm Err}|\le N!\,N2^{N+1}\max_\sigma M_\sigma(\lambda_J/\kappa)\prod_e\|R_e\|$ with $\|R_e\|=\sum_p|\rho_{e,p}/p|$ (the form without $\sum_\sigma|w_\sigma|$ holds only if $\sum_\sigma|w_\sigma(p)|\le1$ for every choice). (c) For each pole choice $\sum_p(-\rho_{e,p}/p)=R_e(0)$ and $\sum_\sigma w_\sigma(p)=1$; if all $\mathcal J_\sigma$ are equal the leading term is $\mathcal J\prod_eR_e(0)$; for $N=1$, (b) is `lem:appO-equal-time`(iii). *Example (even Lorentzian regulator $\chi_K(k)=K^2/(k^2+K^2)$):* the normalized pole weights of $R_e\chi_K$ are $u_z=K^2/(z_e^2+K^2)$, $u_\pm=z_e/(2(z_e\mp iK))$, $u_z+u_++u_-=1$; on the contour $k^+=e^{i\theta}x$, $\theta\le\pi/4$, the final group $\{z_e:x_e>0\}\cup\{-iK\}$ and the initial group $\{z_e:x_e<0\}\cup\{+iK\}$ lie in closed quadrants, so (b) holds with $\sum_\sigma|w_\sigma|\le2$ for $N=2$, and $\sum_p|u_e(p)|\le c_U$ with $c_U\le1+\sqrt2$ (numerically $c_U=1.6485$); for $|z_e|\ll K$ the race weights are recovered, for $|z_e|\gg K$ both regulator poles carry weight $\frac12$. `\uses{lem:appO-joint-localization}`. Proof: full (partial fractions; multilinearity; `lem:appO-joint-localization` uses no relation between $r_e$ and $z_e$). Elementary; ours.
- **引理 `lem:appO-quadrant-residues` — 两条 SS 线的精确 $k^-$ 留数：象限公式** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）The $N=2$ case of `lem:appO-joint-localization`(a) for two SS lines on one $A$-spectator, $z_e=a_e/(2k_e^+)\mp i0$, $a_e=\mathbf k_{Te}^2$: in the quadrant $Q=({\rm sgn}\,k_1^+,{\rm sgn}\,k_2^+)$, $F_A^{[\pi]}(k^+)\equiv\int\frac{d^2k^-}{(2\pi)^2}J_A^{[\pi]}\prod_eR_e=\prod_e(-i\,{\rm sgn}(k_e^+)\,r_e)\,J_A^{(\sigma(\pi,Q))}(z_1,z_2)$, with $\sigma(\pi,Q)$ the sector with initial set $\{e:k_e^+<0\}$ and colour order $\pi$; $F_A^{[\pi]}=0$ if $\pi$ is incompatible with $Q$ ($(+,-)$ kills $[12]$, $(-,+)$ kills $[21]$); in $(-,+)$, $F_A^{[12]}(-k,k)=-\frac1{4k^2}J_A^{(1V2)}(z_1,z_2)$; for $|z_e|\ge2\lambda_A$, $F_A^{[\pi]}=\frac{\mathcal J_A}{a_1a_2}w^{[\pi]}(k^+)[1+O(\lambda_A|k^+|/a)]$ under (EQ); each quadrant piece extends holomorphically to the upper half-plane in $k_e^+$ for $k_e^+>0$ and to the lower one for $k_e^+<0$. `\uses{lem:appO-joint-localization}`. Proof: full. Checks: X1 `test_twoexch_parts2.py`; independent reviewer residue codes ($5\times10^{-14}$). Ours (an explicit instance).
- **引理 `lem:appO-preintegration-localization` — 远离壳的软线：在 $S$ 圈积分之前局域化** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）$j$ a localizable endpoint on a spectator whose soft line ends on an internal vertex of the soft subgraph $S$; the $S$-loop momenta $l_S$ and all other variables fixed and real, $k_j^+\ne0$; every internal line $l=l_0+\epsilon_lk_j$ of $S$ that depends on $k_j^-$ (and the connecting line) satisfies $|l^2(0)-m_l^2|\ge4|l^+|\Lambda_j$ at $k_j^-=0$; $R_j(\cdot;l_S)$ is proper in $k_j^-$. (a) At fixed $l_S$, $R_j$ is a proper rational function of $k_j^-$ whose poles all lie at distance $\ge2\Lambda_j$ from 0; it is holomorphic on $|k_j^-|<\Lambda_j$ and bounded there by $2^{n_j}$ times the product of the moduli of its factors at $k_j^-=0$. (b) At fixed $l_S$: for one localizable insertion `lem:appO-equal-time`(iii) applies with absolute error $O(M\lambda_J\|R_j(\cdot;l_S)\|/\Lambda_j)$; for several insertions `lem:appO-multipole-localization`(b) applies with the factor $\sum_\sigma|w_\sigma(p)|$, bounded by $N!$ if the soft-line poles of each group lie in a closed quadrant and not bounded in general. (c) Hence localization before the $l_S$ integration holds pointwise outside the exceptional set $E_j=\{l_S:$ some soft line carrying $k_j$ has $|l^2-m^2|<4|l^+|\Lambda_j\}$; this does **not** prove the half-plane splitting after the $l_S$ integration, which `asm:appO-mixing` therefore replaces by its clause (a′). `\uses{lem:appO-equal-time, lem:appO-multipole-localization}`. Proof: full ($l^2-m^2$ is affine in $k_j^-$ with slope $2\epsilon_ll^+$). Elementary; ours as a pre-integration replacement.
- **引理 `lem:appO-glauber-operator` — Glauber 插入的非阿贝尔算符结构** [R, R_def]. （$R_{\rm def}$ …）Let $V_G=\sum_{i,j}\chi_{ij}\mathbf T_i\cdot\mathbf T'_j$ with real $\chi_{ij}$, $\mathbf T_i$ ($\mathbf T'_j$) the colour generators of spectator $i$ of $A$ ($j$ of $B$) (the form produced by `prop:appO-ss-localize`(iii); pointer). An ordering convention assigns weights $w(\pi_A,\pi_B)\in\mathbb C$, $\sum w=1$, and $\mathcal A_N=\frac{(-i)^N}{N!}\sum_{(i_e,j_e)}\prod_e\chi_{i_ej_e}\sum_{\pi_A,\pi_B}w(\pi_A,\pi_B)[\prod^{\pi_A}_eT^{a_e}_{i_e}][\prod^{\pi_B}_eT'^{a_e}_{j_e}]$, $\mathcal A_1=-iV_G$. (a) $V_G$ is Hermitian and $\mathcal U_G=e^{-iV_G}$ is unitary (formal power series); (b) abelian: $\mathcal A_N=(-iV_G)^N/N!$ for any weights; (c) common ordering ($w$ supported on $\pi_A=\pi_B$, generated by a common non-negative time distribution): $\sum_N\mathcal A_N={\rm T}\exp(-i\int dt\,V(t))$ with Hermitian $V(t)$, unitary; (d) for one spectator per hadron, $X\equiv\mathbf T_A\cdot\mathbf T_B$, and $p_{\rm opp}$ the total weight of opposite orderings: $\mathcal A_2=\frac{(-i\chi)^2}{2}\big[X^2+p_{\rm opp}\frac{C_A}{2}X\big]$ and $\mathcal A_2+\mathcal A_2^\dagger+\mathcal A_1^\dagger\mathcal A_1=-\frac12C_A\chi^2({\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp})X$ (crossed $-$ ladder $=\frac{C_A}{2}X$; $\sum_{a,b}\{T^a,T^b\}\otimes\{T'^a,T'^b\}=4X^2+C_AX$); (e) with equal weights $w=\frac14$ for the four ordering pairs ($p_{\rm opp}=\frac12$; the strict-window weights of `lem:appO-joint-localization`(e), pointer): $(1+\mathcal A_1+\mathcal A_2)^\dagger(1+\mathcal A_1+\mathcal A_2)=1-\frac14C_A\chi^2X+O(\chi^3)$; on a colour-singlet $q\bar q$ pair ($X=-C_F$) the "probability" is $1+\frac14C_AC_F\chi^2>1$. `% uses: none` (self-contained colour algebra). Proof: full (colour algebra; Dyson series). VF-REF-10. Checks: `refdoc/work_R8/check_colour.py` (SU(2,3,4); F×F, F×F̄, F×A, A×A; common ordering with 2+2 spectators unitary to $4\times10^{-19}$, independent orderings not), `refdoc/work_R7/check_weights_colour.py`. Meaning (print): the residual is controlled by ${\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp}$ alone; the exact two-gluon exchange between eikonal lines gives $p_{\rm opp}=-iY/\pi$ (`prop:dy-eikonal-twogluon`, proof in §O.8), so the strict-window residual of (e) is a **truncation artefact** (ERR-S1).
- **定义 `def:appO-glob-model` — 全局两时模型 $\mathbb M_{\rm glob}$、NF 域、模式与次序权重** (no tag). (a) *Model* (a definition, not an assumption): colour-stripped jet functions $J_A(k^-)$ depending only on the minus components of the insertion momenta and $J_B(p)$, $p=-k^+$, both satisfying (SEC), with exact connecting propagators $R_e(k)=(2k_e^+k_e^--a_e+i0)^{-1}$, $a_e=\mathbf k_{Te}^2$; the model integral of a connection is $\int\prod_e\frac{d^4k_e}{(2\pi)^4}J_A(k^-)\prod_eR_e(k_e)J_B(-k^+)$ over all of $\mathbb R^4$ per line — `lem:appO-glauber-pc`(a),(b) applied globally, also where they fail at relative $O(1)$ ($|k^+|\sim a/\lambda_A$, $|k^-|\sim a/\lambda_B$); for two SS lines $I(\pi_A,\pi_B)=\int\frac{d^2k^+}{(2\pi)^2}F_A^{[\pi_A]}(k^+)J_B^{[\pi_B]}(-k^+)$ ($[\pi]$: the sectors of colour order $\pi$; `lem:appO-quadrant-residues`) is the colour-stripped ladder ($\pi_A=\pi_B$) or crossed ($\pi_A\ne\pi_B$) graph built from $J_A$, $J_B$. (b) *NF domains:* $\mathcal N_X$ is the set of internal configurations of $J_X$ on which every final-state energy difference of $J_X$ is negative (initial ones are negative for bound hadrons); statements "on $\mathcal N_A\cap\mathcal N_B$" are pointwise and exclude an $O(1)$ part of spectator phase space (in the scalar spectator model $\mathbf s_T\cdot\mathbf k_{2T}>\frac12\mathbf k_{2T}^2$ leaves $\mathcal N$). (c) *Patterns:* two amplitude-side SS lines are fully coincident (same $A$-spectator $i$ and same $B$-spectator $j$), half coincident (same $i$, $j_1\ne j_2$) or non-coincident. (d) *Weights:* $N=\sum_{\pi_A,\pi_B}I(\pi_A,\pi_B)$, $W=I/N$, $N_0=\mathcal J_A\mathcal J_B/(a_1a_2)$, $p_{\rm opp}=W(12,21)+W(21,12)$; with $X=\mathbf T_i\cdot\mathbf T_j$ and $\mathcal M^{(1)}=-i\chi X$: $\mathcal M^{(2)}=\frac{(-i\chi)^2}2[X^2+p_{\rm opp}\frac{C_A}2X]$ (`lem:appO-glauber-operator`(d)). (e) *Scales:* $\lambda_X=\max|A|$, $\mu_X=\min|A|$ over the energy differences of $J_X$ ($\mu_X>0$ on $\mathcal N_X$), $a=(a_1a_2)^{1/2}$, $\varepsilon=\lambda_A\lambda_B/a$, $L=\ln(1/\varepsilon)$, $m_X=M_X/|\mathcal J_X|$, $m^\flat_X=M^\flat_X/|\mathcal J_X|$. *Representation dependence:* the $k^-$ integrals of $\mathbb M_{\rm glob}$ evaluate $J_A$ at $|k^-|\sim a/\lambda_B$, where `lem:appO-glauber-pc`(b) fails; physical conclusions from model theorems are conditional on `asm:appO-collinear-ends`. `\uses{def:appO-sectors, lem:appO-glauber-pc, lem:appO-quadrant-residues, lem:appO-glauber-operator}`.
- **定义 `def:appO-regulator-class` — 正规子类 $\mathfrak X$ 与双侧窗口** (no tag). (a) *Class $\mathfrak X$:* a factor $\prod_e\chi(k_e^+)$ with $\chi$ holomorphic in each closed quadrant obtained by rotating a half line counterclockwise by up to $\pi/2$, real on the half lines, $|\chi(k)-1|\le|k|/K_c$ for $|k|\le K_c$ and $|\chi(k)|\le K_c/|k|$ for $|k|\ge K_c$, with $\lambda_B\ll K_c\ll a/\lambda_A$ (example: $\chi=1/(1\pm k/K_c)$ on $k\gtrless0$; sufficient, not necessary). (b) *Even Lorentzian:* $\chi_K(k)=K^2/(k^2+K^2)$, on the $A$-side $k^-$. (c) *Two-sided window:* $\chi^+(k^+)$ at a scale $K^+$ with $\lambda_B\ll K^+\ll a/\lambda_A$ and $\chi^-(k^-)$ at $K^-$ with $\lambda_A\ll K^-\ll a/\lambda_B$, with $K^+K^-\gg a$ so that the near-shell segment $2k^+k^-\sim\mathbf k_T^2$ is retained (the range $a\ll K^+K^-\ll a/\varepsilon$ is non-empty for $\varepsilon\ll1$). `\uses{def:appO-glob-model}`.
- **引理 `lem:appO-kplus-rotation` — $k^+$ 围道的转动** (数学). In $\mathbb M_{\rm glob}$ for two SS lines, with $J_A$, $J_B$ satisfying (SEC) and the configuration on $\mathcal N_B$ (no condition on the final energies of $J_A$): for each colour pair and every $\theta\in[0,\pi/2]$, $I(\pi_A,\pi_B)=e^{2i\theta}\int\frac{d^2\kappa}{(2\pi)^2}F_A^{[\pi_A]}(e^{i\theta}\kappa)J_B^{[\pi_B]}(-e^{i\theta}\kappa)$, each quadrant piece of $F_A$ continued from its real quadrant; at $\theta=\pi/2$, $I=-\int\frac{d^2\kappa}{(2\pi)^2}F_A(i\kappa)J_B(-i\kappa)$; the same holds with class-$\mathfrak X$ regulators on $k^+$ and rational regulators on the $A$-side $k^-$. `\uses{def:appO-glob-model, def:appO-regulator-class, lem:appO-quadrant-residues}`. Proof: full — holomorphy on the swept sets at fixed $\eta>0$ (the zeros of the $J_B$ factors lie in quadrants that are not swept, using $\mathcal N_B$ for the final ones); the radial identity $\frac d{dt}[e^{2it}G(e^{it}\kappa)]=i\,{\rm div}_\kappa(\kappa\,e^{2it}G(e^{it}\kappa))$ and the divergence theorem (only the arcs contribute, bounded by the crude (SEC) bounds: $O(R^{-2})$, strips $O(R^{-2}\ln R)$, axis bands $O(R^{-1}\ln R)$); $\eta\to0$ at $\theta=\pi/2$ by dominated convergence ($|A+i(\eta-y)|\ge\langle y\rangle/\sqrt5$ for $\eta\le\mu_B$); the range stops at $\pi/2$ (at $t=\pi$ an initial factor vanishes). Checks: rotated vs real contour to 7–10 digits; $\theta$-independence to 8 digits. Ours.
- **引理 `lem:appO-ray-identity` — 射线恒等式** (数学). Let $\rho$ be holomorphic in $0<\arg k<\pi/2$, continuous up to the boundary except for finitely many simple poles $k_p$ on the positive real axis (where the boundary values $\rho(k+i0)$ are taken), with $|\rho(k)-1|\le C|k|/k_1$ for $|k|\le k_1$ and $|\rho(k)|\le Ck_2/|k|$ for $|k|\ge k_2$ in the closed quadrant. Then $\int_0^\infty\frac{d\kappa}\kappa{\rm Im}\,\rho(i\kappa)=-\frac\pi2+{\rm Im}\int_0^\infty\frac{dk}k\rho(k+i0)$; if $\rho$ is real on the positive axis away from its poles, with real residues, this equals $-\frac\pi2-\pi\sum_p{\rm Res}_{k_p}\rho/k_p$, and without poles exactly $-\frac\pi2$. `% uses: none`. Proof: full (Cauchy's theorem for $[\rho(k)-(1+k)^{-1}]/k$ on the quadrant, axis poles indented from inside). Elementary; its role in `prop:appO-twoexch-spectator` is ours.
- **引理 `lem:appO-two-insertion-bounds` — 转动围道上双插入喷注函数的界** (数学). $J$ satisfies (SEC) with $N=2$, (EQ) and (ADJ), and all final energies of $J$ are negative; at $\theta=\pi/2$, $p_e=-i\kappa_e$, $f_\pi(\kappa)=J^{[\pi]}(-i\kappa)$, $S=f_{12}+f_{21}$, $R=\max(r_1,r_2)$, $r_P=|\kappa_1+\kappa_2|$, $\langle y\rangle=(\mu^2+y^2)^{1/2}$. (a) $|i/(A-iy)|\le1/\langle y\rangle$ and $|i/(A-iy)-i/(-iy)|\le\lambda/(|y|\langle y\rangle)$; (b) $|J^{(\sigma)}|\le M^\flat_\sigma/(\langle y_1\rangle\langle y_2\rangle)$; (c) off strip ($R\ge4\lambda$, $r_P\ge R/4$): $|f_\pi|\le400M^\flat\lambda/(R^2\langle\min(r_1,r_2)\rangle)$; (d) strip ($R\ge4\lambda$, $r_P<R/4$): $f_{12}=g(-i\kappa_P)/\kappa_2+E_{12}$, $f_{21}=-g(-i\kappa_P)/\kappa_2+E_{21}$, $|E_\pi|\le16M^\flat\lambda/(R^2\langle r_P\rangle)$, with one function $g$ for both orders by (ADJ); (e) $|g(-iU)|\le\min(2M^\flat/\langle U\rangle,4M\lambda/U^2)$ and $\int_{\mathbb R}g(-iU)\frac{dU}{2\pi}=-i\mathcal J$; (f) $S\in L^1(\mathbb R^2)$ and $\int S\frac{d^2\kappa}{(2\pi)^2}=-\mathcal J$; (g) for $K\ge4\lambda$, $l=\ln(1+4K/\mu)$: $\int_{R\le K}|f_\pi|\le12M^\flat l^2$, $\int_{R>K,\,r_P\ge R/4}|f_\pi|\le3200M^\flat(\lambda/K)(1+l)$, $\int_{R>K,\,r_P<R/4}|E_\pi|\le86M^\flat(\lambda/K)(1+l)$, $|\int_{R>K}S|\le6600M^\flat(\lambda/K)(1+l)$; (h) on the $\pi/4$ contour the same with every constant multiplied by 8. `\uses{def:appO-sectors, lem:appO-kplus-rotation}`. Proof: full (telescoping; pairing of adjacent sectors through (ADJ) in the axis bands and strips; cancellation of the eikonal products by (EQ); residues of $g$). Checks: random factorized and non-factorized (ADJ)-class jets (maximal ratios to the bounds $\le0.52$); without (ADJ) the strip tails do not cancel. The proven constants are crude (numerically about 6 and 0.4 instead of 400 and 16). Ours (elementary).
- **引理 `lem:appO-halfcoinc-colour` — 半重合对的色代数** [R, R_def]. （$R_{\rm def}$ …）Half-coincident pair (spectator $i$ of $A$; spectators $j_1\ne j_2$ of $B$), $X_e=\mathbf T_i\cdot\mathbf T_{j_e}$, one exchange $\mathcal M^{(1)}_e=-ic_eX_e$ on the amplitude side with $c_e$ real ($+ic_eX_e$ on the conjugate side). (a) With $W_{12}+W_{21}=1$ the $O(c_1c_2)$ residual of the side sum is $\mathcal R=2ic_1c_2\,{\rm Im}W_{12}\,[X_1,X_2]$, $[X_1,X_2]=if^{abc}T^c_iT^a_{j_1}T^b_{j_2}$ (the sign flips if $c_e$ denotes the complex coefficient of $\mathcal M^{(1)}_e=c_eX_e$). (b) $[X_1,X_2]=0$ on the colour-singlet subspace of $(i,j_1,j_2)$ alone. (c) For Drell–Yan remnant states (active parton of $A$ in $3$ or $\bar3$; remnants coupled to a singlet): $\langle\psi|[X_1,X_2]|\psi\rangle=-\langle\psi|(K_i\otimes1)\,if^{abc}T^a_{j_1}T^b_{j_2}\sum_{k\in{\rm B\text{-}rem}\setminus\{j_1,j_2\}}T^c_k|\psi\rangle$, which **vanishes for every $\psi$ when the remnant of $B$ is $\{j_1,j_2\}$** (e.g. valence $p\bar p$). (d) In a common real weight basis every $X_e$ is real symmetric and $[X_1,X_2]$ real antisymmetric, so the expectation vanishes for every real colour state (${\rm Tr}(\rho[X_1,X_2])=0$ for real density matrices). A non-zero value needs a further remnant parton in the hadron of $j_1,j_2$ **and** complex relative phases between real singlet couplings; generic multi-parton remnants with momentum-dependent complex colour amplitudes are not covered, and no symmetry forcing the remnant-integrated residual to vanish is known (open). (c) uses $\dim{\rm Hom}(8\otimes R_A,R_A)=1$ (gluon-active hadrons not covered). `\uses{lem:appO-glauber-operator}`. Proof: full (amplitude-side, conjugate-side and mixed terms; Wigner–Eckart and colour conservation; real structure). Checks: X1 `checks_C2_C3_C4.py`, `fix_r1_scripts/colour_dy_check.py`, `check_halfcoinc_singlets_v2.py`; independent codes in random real bases. Ours as statements; the colour-singlet-target mechanism is that of `Lindsay:1983dt`; context: the analogous $f^{abc}TTT$ structure of `CataniDeFlorianRodrigo2012` (its contribution to squared amplitudes vanishes by colour conservation for four partons) and `ForshawSeymourSiodmok2012`.
- **定义 `def:appO-family` — 旁观者交换族与积分映射** (no tag). $(\Gamma_0,R_0)\in\mathcal G_0$ without SS lines (the *core*). The *family* $\mathfrak F(\Gamma_0)$ of a given order: all forward graphs obtained by adding any number of SS lines to the spectator partons of $\Gamma_0$ (each on the amplitude or conjugate side), up to that order; $R_\Gamma$ = $R_0$ plus all SS lines soft. A *connection* $p$ is a multiset of SS lines with their partons $(i_l,j_l)$; a *side assignment* gives each line a side; $\Theta^{\rm SS}_\Gamma=\prod_{l\in{\rm SS}}\Theta_l$; $\mathfrak P_\Gamma$ integrates all four components of all SS momenta in $\Theta^{\rm SS}_\Gamma\tilde F_{\Gamma,R_\Gamma}$, giving a function on the space of $\Gamma_0$. $p$ is *coincident* if two localized insertions (SS, or SS with SA/AS) sit on the same spectator parton (any sides). Parts with some SS momentum outside its window belong to $K_{R_\Gamma}$ or to other regions of $\Gamma$ (SS line collinear or hard) and are handled by App N's subtractions. *Side-independent windows* (slot X1c): let $J_A^0$ be the uncut forward $A$-subgraph with the SS-insertion vertices removed (defined on the forward graph, hence independent of the cut), route each SS momentum in the standard flow from its $A$-end through $J_A$ to $V_H$ ($V_H^\dagger$), let $L_A^{\rm SS}$ be the set of lines of $J_A^0$ carrying SS momentum under at least one side assignment, $x^A_{\min}=\min_{l\in L_A^{\rm SS}}l^+/P_A^+$ (a momentum fraction, independent of side and cut), $n_A$ the maximal number of SS-carrying lines in one state, $E^{(0)}(l)$ the on-shell energy at zero SS momenta, $\hat\nu_A=P_A^-+2\sum_{l\in L(J_A^0)}E^{(0)}(l)+2n_A(\sum_m|\mathbf k_{Tm}|)^2/(x^A_{\min}P_A^+)$, $\hat\nu_B$ likewise ($A\leftrightarrow B$, plus $\leftrightarrow$ minus), and $\hat\Theta_m=\vartheta\big(4\hat\nu_A\hat\nu_B/(\lambda^{a_G}\mathbf k_{Tm}^2)\big)$; the SS windows of the family are the $\hat\Theta_m$, and $\Theta^{\rm SS}_\Gamma=\prod_m\hat\Theta_m$. `\uses{def:appO-leading-data, def:appO-windows}`.
- **引理 `lem:appO-side-independent-windows` — 与侧分配无关的窗口消去窗口边缘项** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appO-lcopt}。（本引理只涉及部分子态。）With the windows $\hat\Theta_m$ of `def:appO-family`: (a) $\hat\nu_A$, $\hat\nu_B$ are independent of the side assignment and of the cut; (b) for every side assignment and every state $S$ of $J_A$, $|A_D|\le\hat\nu_A$ for initial and $|A'_D|\le\hat\nu_A$ for final states; (c) on $\{x^A_{\min}\ge c,\ x^B_{\min}\ge c\}$ ($c>0$ fixed) $\hat\nu\sim\lambda^2Q$ in the Glauber scaling region, so $\hat\Theta=1$ on the Glauber core; the complement — wee lines carrying SS momentum, $x_{\min}\to0$, where $\lambda_J\le C\hat\nu$ degrades the localization error — is assigned to the soft-angle region $K_R$, i.e. to `asm:appO-deformation`(c); (d) the localization bounds of `prop:appO-ss-localize` hold with $\lambda_J\le C\hat\nu$, and the family identity $\sum_{\rm sides}\prod_l(\cdots)=\prod_l(-iG_lX_l+iG_lX_l)=0$ holds at every point of the support, so the window-edge terms of `prop:appO-ss-cancel`(iv) **vanish identically**; the out-of-window parts are not removed (they enlarge $K_R$, `asm:appO-deformation`(c)). `\uses{asm:appO-lcopt, def:appO-family, def:appO-windows, prop:appO-ss-localize, lem:appO-orientation}`. Proof: full — (a) by construction (zero-SS-momentum energies, extremal $x_{\min}$ and $n_A$ over all side assignments, everything on the forward graph); (b) $E(l)\le2E^{(0)}(l)+\Delta_l^2/l^+$ ($\Delta_l$ the SS transverse momentum carried by $l$, $l^+$ unchanged), at most one segment of each line of $J_A^0$ and at most $n_A$ SS-carrying lines per state, and the same bound for the cut energy; (c) power counting; (d) the family algebra needs only side-independent window factors. Slot X1c: PASS (recommended by both final reviewers), with the grade [R, R_cond] instead of the planned [R, R_PT] (the premise is that of `prop:appO-ss-cancel`). Ours.
- **命题 `prop:appO-ss-cancel` — 旁观者–旁观者 Glauber 交换在族求和后相消** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appO-lcopt}。（本命题只涉及部分子态。）For a family $\mathfrak F(\Gamma_0)$ with $J_A$, $J_B$ cut-summed (`lem:appO-unitarity`) and the non-SS soft endpoints localized or initial-only: (i) for non-coincident connections, on ${\rm supp}\,\Theta^{\rm SS}$, each SS line becomes a $\mp iV_G$ insertion at $\tau=0$ with the fibre-integrated error of `prop:appO-ss-localize`; (ii) $\rho_A$, $\rho_B$ act only on spectators and commute with $V_H$ and with $J_B$'s hard vertex (SS insertions before or after $V_H$ agree); (iii) for each non-coincident connection $p$ ($|p|\ge1$), summing over side assignments and cuts and applying $\mathfrak P$ gives **zero on the window core** up to the error of (i): $\sum_s\prod_{l:s_l={\rm conj}}(+iG_lX_l)\prod_{l:s_l={\rm amp}}(-iG_lX_l)=\prod_{l=1}^N(-iG_lX_l+iG_lX_l)=0$ ($X_l=t^a_{i_l}\otimes t^a_{j_l}$; the identity on $\mathcal H_A\otimes\mathcal H_B$ stands between the two sides because the cuts of $J_A$ and $J_B$ move independently and window Glauber lines are never cut); in the abelian theory all connections are non-coincident and $\sum_{\Gamma\in\mathfrak F}\mathfrak P_\Gamma[\Theta^{\rm SS}_\Gamma\tilde F_{\Gamma,R_\Gamma}]=\tilde F_{\Gamma_0,R_0}+(\text{fibre-integrated error})+(\text{window-edge terms})$; (iv) with the side-independent windows of `def:appO-family` the window-edge terms vanish identically (`lem:appO-side-independent-windows`(d)); the out-of-window parts and the wee-line endpoint region belong to the soft-angle region (`asm:appO-deformation`(c), §O.7 — pointer); (v) coincident connections (non-abelian) are not covered: with the joint weights of `lem:appO-joint-localization` the localized expression is defined, but the side sum need not vanish; its $O(\chi^2)$ structure is `lem:appO-glauber-operator`; for the coincident $O(g^4)$ patterns see the model results `prop:appO-twoexch-spectator` and `rem:appO-halfcoinc` below. *Alternative proof of (iii) for commuting insertions* (all orders, any number of SS lines and commuting coincidences): by `lem:appO-joint-localization`(a),(c) (`lem:appO-multipole-localization` for multi-pole factors) the localized value is $\prod_eR_e(0)\sum_\sigma w_\sigma\mathcal J_\sigma=\prod_eR_e(0)\mathcal J(0)$, since commuting insertions have order-independent equal-time values and $\sum_\sigma w_\sigma=1$; the error estimate for multi-pole factors is taken from the line-by-line localization. `\uses{asm:appO-lcopt, lem:appO-unitarity, prop:appO-ss-localize, lem:appO-joint-localization, lem:appO-glauber-operator, def:appO-family, lem:appO-side-independent-windows}`. Proof: full on window cores (commutativity and completeness only). The integrated form (over the core variables) needs `asm:appO-envelope` and is part of `prop:appO-one-sided`/`thm:appO-main`. Error type: E-F. Generalizes `prop:dy-abelian-model` (arbitrary $J_A$, $J_B$, any number of spectators).
- **例 `ex:appO-single-exchange` — 单条 Glauber 交换** . Spectator model of `ex:dy-spectator-lcopt`: $J_A(k^-)=\frac{i}{D_1}\big[\frac{i}{k^--\alpha+i0}+\frac{i}{\beta-k^-+i0}\big]$, $\beta-\alpha=D_1$; $\mathcal J_-=\mathcal J_+=i/D_1$; $\int\frac{dk^-}{2\pi}J_AR=R(0)\,i/D_1$ (no extra denominator; the exchange happens at $\tau=0$ and transfers only $\mathbf k_T$); amplitude $\to-i\chi(b)\mathbf T_A\cdot\mathbf T_B\mathcal M^{(0)}(b)$, conjugate $+i\chi$, cut sum $0$ (Parseval: same $b$ on both sides); single orderings are cutoff dependent ($\frac12$ each under symmetric truncation, $\theta(\pm k^+)$ with the full propagator), their sum is not. SA: the $J_A$ end localized, the $J_B$ end initial-only (deformable along $k^+\to k^+-i\kappa$); AS: $J_A=J^{\rm in}$, $\mathcal J_+=0\ne\mathcal J_-$, localizing would give $\theta(-k^+)R(0)\mathcal J_-$ — the contact term; therefore the rewriting does not localize endpoints on absorbed active lines. Figure: redraw of the four cut–ordering configurations (in `fig:appO-cutsum` panel b, or a separate panel). `\uses{lem:appO-equal-time, lem:appO-insertion-values, prop:appO-ss-localize}`. Checks: `refdoc/work_R8/check_kinematics.py` K2–K4.
- **例 `ex:appO-two-exchange` — 两条 Glauber 交换与 $p_{\rm opp}$** . One spectator per hadron; ladder colour $X^2$, crossed $X^2+\frac12C_AX$, one exchange on each side $X^2$ (no ordering ambiguity); $\mathcal A_2$ and the unitarity defect of `lem:appO-glauber-operator`(d). Three cases: abelian (zero for any $p_{\rm opp}$); common ordering $p_{\rm opp}=0$ (realized by the Rothstein–Stewart regulator $|2k^z|^{-\eta}$: crossed Glauber boxes vanish, ladders exponentiate to a Hermitian colour phase, `RothsteinStewart2016`); strict window $p_{\rm opp}=\frac12$, residual $-\frac14C_A\chi^2X$. **Exact eikonal value** (§O.8): $p_{\rm opp}=-iY/\pi$, ${\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp}=0$: the whole $s$-channel weight sits in the uncrossed graph, the crossed graph contributes only the reggeization phase $\propto C_AY$; the "missing" $+\frac14C_A\chi^2X$ of the strict truncation is the principal-value part of the same graphs outside the window (ERR-S1; corrected sign ERR-m27). For composite spectators in the global two-time model the uncut-line side sum of this pattern cancels (`prop:appO-twoexch-spectator`); open: the cut direct lines (`rem:appO-cut-ss`), Glauber–soft mixing through Lipatov vertices (`asm:appO-mixing`(c), §O.6 — pointer; slot X1e not delivered) and $O(g^6)$ (`prop:appO-g6-ladder`). `\uses{lem:appO-glauber-operator, prop:dy-eikonal-twogluon}`. Figure: pointer to `fig:dy-two-gluon-exchange` (owner Ch12).
- **假设 `asm:appO-adjacent-swap` — 相邻交换对称性（ADJ）** [\tagP]. Full text in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. Content: every leading-power cut-summed LCOPT jet factor $J_X$ ($X=A,B$) of QCD graphs with two amplitude-side insertions on spectator partons satisfies (ADJ) of `def:appO-sectors`. `\uses{def:appO-sectors}`.
- **假设 `asm:appO-real-jets` — 旁观者喷注函数的实性（REAL）** [\tagP]. Full text in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. Content: (REAL) of `def:appO-sectors` holds for the leading-power cut-summed LCOPT jet factors of QCD graphs with spectator insertions. `\uses{def:appO-sectors}`.
- **假设 `asm:appO-collinear-ends` — 共线端点的匹配** [\tagA]. Full text in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. Content: the SS windows carry the two-sided analytic rapidity regulators of `def:appO-regulator-class`(c); the complements — the $A$-collinear end ($|k^+|\gtrsim K^+$) and the $B$-collinear end ($|k^-|\gtrsim K^-$), together with the cut contributions located there — are assigned to the collinear regions, where collinear factorization (Wilson lines and subtraction terms) reproduces them with no leftover Hermitian $C_AX$ term (fully coincident pattern) and no leftover $[X_1,X_2]$ term (half-coincident pattern). It **relocates**, rather than removes, the content of `asm:appO-mixing`(c) for the coincident $O(g^4)$ patterns; it is not a premise of `thm:appO-main` or `thm:dy-factorization`. `\uses{def:appO-regulator-class, def:appO-glob-model}`.
- **命题 `prop:appO-twoexch-spectator` — 全重合 SS 对的 $O(g^4)$ 侧求和（未割线）：全局两时模型中的相消** [P]. 本命题是关于全局两时模型 $\mathbb M_{\rm glob}$（`def:appO-glob-model`）的陈述：在 $\mathcal N_A\cap\mathcal N_B$ 上逐点、$O(g^4)$、两条**未割**的 SS 线连接同一个 $A$ 旁观者 $i$ 与同一个 $B$ 旁观者 $j$（振幅侧），且满足下述 (iv)(v)。Hypotheses: (i) $\mathbb M_{\rm glob}$ with exact connecting propagators; (ii) $J_A$ satisfies (SEC), (EQ), (REAL), and $J_B$ satisfies (SEC), (EQ), (ADJ), (REAL); (iii) the configuration lies in $\mathcal N_A\cap\mathcal N_B$; (iv) $\varepsilon\le\min(\frac1{16},a_{\min}^2/(4a_1a_2))$; (v) $\delta_Y\le\frac12$ (an explicit combination of the error terms, not implied by (iv)). (a) *(model-internal; complete proof)* $|N/N_0-1|\le C_N\varepsilon^{1/2}(1+L_B)^2$, $|{\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp}|\le C_T\varepsilon^{1/2}(1+L_B)^2(1+L)$ and $|{\rm Im}\,p_{\rm opp}+\frac1\pi\ln\frac1\varepsilon|\le C_I$, with $C_N,C_T,C_I$ explicit polynomials in $m_A,m^\flat_A,m^\flat_B,m_B,c_\rho,a_{\max}/a_{\min}$ and the logarithms $\ell_A=\ln(1+\lambda_A/\mu_A)$, $\ell_B=\ln(1+16\lambda_B/\mu_B)$, $L_B=\ln(1+4\varepsilon^{-1/2}\lambda_B/\mu_B)$; hence the uncut-line side-sum residual $-\frac12C_A\chi^2({\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp})X$ is power suppressed, and the $C_A$ term of $\mathcal M^{(2)}$ equals $(-i\chi X)(-C_A\chi\ln(1/\varepsilon)/(4\pi))$ at leading-logarithmic accuracy — a real multiple of the anti-Hermitian one-exchange amplitude (the gluon reggeization phase). (b) *(QCD jets)* for leading-power cut-summed LCOPT jets of QCD graphs (SEC), (EQ) hold under `asm:appO-lcopt`, and (ADJ), (REAL) are `asm:appO-adjacent-swap`, `asm:appO-real-jets`; (a) holds for them conditionally (content [P], set by the tags of these entries); in the scalar spectator model all hypotheses (ii) hold exactly. (c) *(physical reading)* within App O's framework the side sum of the **uncut** direct lines cancels once the window is combined with the adjacent near-shell region of the same lines: the $-\frac12$ is generated at the $A$-collinear end on the rotated contour, i.e. in the near-mass-shell region $2k^+k^-\sim\mathbf k_T^2$ on the real contour (absent with the strict propagator, `prop:appO-strict-window-shape`), so the physical content needs `asm:appO-collinear-ends`; the cut direct lines (real emission, inside $\mathcal G_0$) are not included (`rem:appO-cut-ss`), so this does **not** establish the $\mathcal G_0$-restricted complete-set clause and does not refute `asm:appO-mixing`(c); nothing is claimed for the spectator-integrated cross section. `\uses{def:appO-glob-model, lem:appO-joint-localization, lem:appO-quadrant-residues, lem:appO-kplus-rotation, lem:appO-ray-identity, lem:appO-two-insertion-bounds, lem:appO-glauber-operator, asm:appO-lcopt, asm:appO-adjacent-swap, asm:appO-real-jets, asm:appO-collinear-ends}`. Proof (model-internal, full; X1 report, Theorem T2): rotation to $k^+=i\kappa$ (`lem:appO-kplus-rotation`); regions: inner square $R\le K=\lambda_B\varepsilon^{-1/2}$, strips $r_P<R/4$, off strip; $A$-side localization on the rotated contour (`lem:appO-joint-localization`(f)) with real localized weights; `lem:appO-two-insertion-bounds` for $J_B$; the inner-square term with $v=w^{[12]}-\frac12$ is purely imaginary by (REAL) and the oddness of $v$; in the strips the $g$-terms give $-\frac i{2\pi}\int_K^\infty\frac{d\kappa}\kappa[\varrho_{12}+\varrho_{21}]$, and `lem:appO-ray-identity` gives exactly $-\frac\pi2$ for each ray integral on $\mathcal N_A$ with (REAL), so the real parts cancel up to the explicit error terms; division by $N/N_0$ under (v). Numerics (scalar spectator model): ${\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp}=0.0123,\,0.0021,\,0.00035$ at $\lambda=0.01,0.003,0.001$, $N/N_0\to1$, ${\rm Im}\,p_{\rm opp}+L/\pi$ bounded; non-factorized jet pairs: ${\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp}\to0$ roughly as $\varepsilon^{0.63}$, $\varepsilon^{0.70}$, $\varepsilon^{0.73}$ for the three pairs (exponents as re-fitted in the final review; the report quotes $\varepsilon^{0.7}$–$\varepsilon^{0.77}$). **地位与缺口.** [P] as a QCD statement (model theorem, WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.3 item 4): the model-internal statement (a) is fully proved; the QCD transfer (b) rests on the \tagP entries; the physical reading (c) rests on `asm:appO-collinear-ends` and on the open cut contributions. Prior art: the physical $O(\alpha_s^2)$ cancellation for this pattern in explicit two-loop model calculations — `Lindsay:1983ny` (meson + meson, Feynman gauge, all graphs) and `Brandt:1988xt` (eikonal "mesons"); the reggeization reading is textbook (`Fadin:1975cb`, `KuraevLipatovFadin1977`, `RothsteinStewart2016`). Ours: the derivation for general LCOPT jets with explicit estimates, the ray-identity mechanism and the regulator analysis (it extends ERR-S1 from eikonal lines to composite spectators); no novelty marker (the check of `CollinsSoperSterman1988` and `Collins2011` §14.4 for non-commuting coincident exchanges is open). Slot X1f: PARTIAL (inserted as a model theorem; the first sentence states the restrictions).
- **命题 `prop:appO-twoexch-regulated` — 正规化版本、端点极点与双侧正规子** [P]. 本命题是关于全局两时模型 $\mathbb M_{\rm glob}$ 的陈述（$O(g^4)$，全重合 SS 对，未割线）。(a) *(model-internal; complete proof)* under the hypotheses of `prop:appO-twoexch-spectator` except $\mathcal N_A$, with a class-$\mathfrak X$ regulator $\prod_e\chi(k_e^+)$, $\lambda_B\ll K_c\ll a/\lambda_A$: ${\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp}=O\big((\lambda_B/K_c)^{1/2}+K_c\lambda_A/a\big)$ up to logarithms (mirror statement with a $k^-$ regulator on $\mathcal N_A$); the real part is regulator independent only for regulators that retain the near-shell segment. (b) *(end poles; model-internal, complete proof)* without a regulator and without $\mathcal N_A$ (on $\mathcal N_B$): ${\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp}=-\frac12\sum_p{\rm Res}_{k_p}(\rho_{12}+\rho_{21})/k_p+O(\text{power})$; only the final-gap energies of the mixed sectors enter; the poles sit at the $A$-collinear end $k^+\sim a/\lambda_A$ — artefacts of $\mathbb M_{\rm glob}$ for massive spectators, genuine $A$-collinear configurations for massless ones (assigned to the collinear region by `asm:appO-collinear-ends`, or removed by (a)); in the scalar spectator model ${\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp}=\frac12a_1\alpha_1/(a_1\alpha_1+a_2|d_{10}|)$ if $\alpha_1>0$ (numerically 0.2520 against the predicted 0.2517 at $\lambda=0.001$).
