(c) *(two-sided analytic regulators; numerical)* with $\chi^+(k^+)$ at $K^+$ and an even, line-identical $\chi^-(k^-)$ at $K^-$: ${\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp}\to0$ when the near-shell segment is retained ($K^+K^-\gg\mathbf k_T^2$) and $\to\frac12$ when it is excluded ($K^+K^-\ll\mathbf k_T^2$; the value then depends on the regulator shape, `prop:appO-strict-window-shape`), through a crossover (${\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp}=0.149$ at $K^+K^-/a=1$); the cut contributions are not part of this computation. `\uses{prop:appO-twoexch-spectator, def:appO-regulator-class, lem:appO-multipole-localization, lem:appO-ray-identity, asm:appO-collinear-ends}`. Proof: (a), (b) as for `prop:appO-twoexch-spectator` with the regulator inserted (`lem:appO-ray-identity` with axis poles for (b)); (c) numerical (X1 `fix_r1_scripts/regulator_indep.py`, reproduced on the real contour by a reviewer). **地位与缺口.** [P] as a QCD statement (model theorem): (a), (b) are fully proved in the model, (c) is numerical; the physical reading rests on `asm:appO-collinear-ends`. Ours (no novelty marker).
- **命题 `prop:appO-strict-window-shape` — 严格窗口及其对正规子形状的依赖** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$：在 $O(g^4)$ 对所述局域化积分严格。）（本命题只涉及部分子态。）Strict-Glauber limit (near-shell region excluded; both jets eikonal-localized) with independent regulators $\chi^X_1,\chi^X_2$ on jet $X$ as functions of the light-cone energy injected into jet $X$ ($q=k^-$ for $A$, $q=p=-k^+$ for $B$): with $\delta_X=\frac1{2\pi}{\rm PV}\!\int\chi^X_1(q)\chi^X_2(-q)\frac{dq}q\big/(\chi^X_1(0)\chi^X_2(0))$ one has $w^X_{12}=\frac12+i\delta_X$ and $p_{\rm opp}=\frac12+2\delta_A\delta_B$ (with $k^+$ instead of $p$ as the $B$ variable, $p_{\rm opp}=\frac12-2\delta_A\delta_B^{(k^+)}$). Consequences: $p_{\rm opp}=\frac12$ iff $\delta_A\delta_B=0$ (in particular for even or line-identical regulators on one side — `lem:appO-joint-localization`(e)); independent sharp windows give any real $p_{\rm opp}$ ($\delta=\ln(\min(K_2,K'_1)/\min(K_1,K'_2))/(2\pi)$); non-even line-dependent rational regulators give complex $\delta$ (e.g. $w_{12}=97/105$). Excluding the near-shell region decorrelates the orderings in the two jets, and what remains is fixed by the regulators alone: $\frac12$ is the symmetric-regulator value, not a universal one. `\uses{lem:appO-joint-localization, lem:appO-multipole-localization, lem:appO-glauber-operator}`. Proof: full (the three-sector identity $J^{[12]}_{\rm eik}=\mathcal J\,2\pi\delta(q_1+q_2)\,i/(q_1+i0)$ by (EQ); $i/(q+i0)=i\,{\rm PV}(1/q)+\pi\delta(q)$; factorization in the strict limit); exact check by iterated residues (X1 `final_scripts/p2_orientation.py`). The symmetric case is the earlier statement; the formula and the classification are ours (elementary).
- **命题 `prop:appO-pergraph-weights` — 全重合对的逐图次序权重不由窗口数据决定** [P]. 本命题是关于全局两时模型 $\mathbb M_{\rm glob}$ 中所述喷注函数类的陈述（$O(g^4)$，全重合对，未割线）。Class: $J_A$ with (SEC), (EQ), (REAL) on $\mathcal N_A$; $J_B$ with (SEC), (EQ), (ADJ), (REAL) on $\mathcal N_B$; window data $D=(D_{\rm win}(J_A),D_{\rm win}(J_B),a_e,\mathbf k_{Te})$, $D_{\rm win}(J_X)=\{\mathcal J_\sigma\ \text{for all sectors};\lambda_X\}$. (a) *(model, proved)* $W(12,21)=W^{\rm in}+W^{\rm out}$ with $W^{\rm out}=-\frac i{2\pi}\int_K^\infty\frac{d\kappa}\kappa\varrho_{12}(\kappa)+O(\lambda_B/K)$ and $W^{\rm in}$ depending on $J_A$ only through $D$ up to $O(\lambda_AK/a_{\min})$. (b) *(model, proved)* adding to the mixed sector $1V2$ of $J_A$ the two-state term $s\,c_2\frac i{A_1+k_1}\frac i{A_2+k_1}\frac i{A'-k_2}$ ($c_2=-i\tilde c_2$, $\tilde c_2/\mathcal J_A$ real, $|A_{1,2}|\le\lambda_A$, $A_1,A_2,A'<0$) preserves (SEC), (EQ), (REAL) and $D$ (it breaks (ADJ) for $J_A$, which the class does not require) and shifts $W(12,21)$ by a purely imaginary amount that is **non-zero whenever $\tilde c_2\ne0$**. (c) *(numerical)* with in-window regulators the $A$-side out-of-window dependence is suppressed, but the per-graph weights still depend on the in-window shape of the mixed sectors of $J_B$ (a shift $-0.0287i$ in an explicit example that leaves $D_{\rm win}(J_B)$, (EQ), (ADJ), (REAL) unchanged). Consequence: within the class no per-graph jet-local rewriting by window data can satisfy per-graph weak equivalence for coincident pairs; all shifts are purely imaginary in the side sum, so ${\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp}$ is unaffected, and `asm:appO-envelope` must be imposed on side-summed families for coincident connections. `\uses{def:appO-glob-model, lem:appO-quadrant-residues, prop:appO-twoexch-spectator, asm:appO-adjacent-swap, asm:appO-real-jets}`. Proof: (a), (b) Step 4 of the proof of `prop:appO-twoexch-spectator` per colour pair with `lem:appO-quadrant-residues`; Cauchy's theorem for the shift integral (the shift density has a fixed sign on $(0,\infty)$); (c) numerical (X1 `fix_r2_scripts/p4_regulated_B.py`). **地位与缺口.** [P] as a QCD statement: (a), (b) are statements about the defined class in the model with complete proofs; whether graph-generated QCD jets realize the perturbation of (b) is open; QCD membership as in `prop:appO-twoexch-spectator`(b). Ours; it sharpens ERR-S3.
- **注 `rem:appO-halfcoinc` — 半重合对：无正规子的两时模型** (no tag). In $\mathbb M_{\rm glob}$, on $\mathcal N_B$, at $O(g^4)$, for two **uncut** SS lines $i$–$j_1$, $i$–$j_2$ ($j_1\ne j_2$) without a rapidity regulator: the side-sum residual is $\mathcal R=2ic_1c_2\,{\rm Im}W_{12}[X_1,X_2]$ (`lem:appO-halfcoinc-colour`(a)) with ${\rm Im}W_{12}=O(1)$ in general (scalar model: $W_{12}\to0.5-0.030559i$; symmetric configurations ${\rm Im}W_{12}=0$; model numerics [P]); its colour factor vanishes for valence remnants and real colour states, including the model's own configuration (`lem:appO-halfcoinc-colour`(c),(d)); the coincident SA patterns ($i$–$j_e$ together with $i$–$b$) carry the same colour structure after colour conservation and were not included; only uncut lines are included (`rem:appO-cut-ss`); for exact on-shell lines the residual is expected to be absent (`rem:appO-onshell-unitarity`, not certified). The fate in QCD is the matching of `asm:appO-collinear-ends` at the $k^-$ end, together with the cut contributions (open, Ch25). The configuration does not occur in `Lindsay:1983ny`, `Brandt:1988xt` (one spectator per hadron) or `Lindsay:1983dt` (no $A$-spectator).
- **命题 `prop:appO-halfcoinc-sensitivity` — 半重合对：$W_{12}$ 的实部与对 $k^-$ 正规子的敏感性** [P]. 本命题是关于全局两时模型 $\mathbb M_{\rm glob}$ 的陈述（在 $\mathcal N_B$ 上、$O(g^4)$、两条未割的 SS 线 $i$–$j_1$、$i$–$j_2$）。Hypotheses: $J_A$ with (SEC), (EQ); $J_B$ with (SEC), (EQ), (ADJ), (REAL); $\varepsilon\ll1$ with smallness conditions as in `prop:appO-twoexch-spectator`. (a) *(model, proved)* $W_{12}=\frac12-\frac1{\mathcal J_B}\int v(\kappa)f_B(\kappa)\frac{d^2\kappa}{(2\pi)^2}+E_b$ with a purely imaginary integral and $|E_b|\le C_b\varepsilon(1+L')^2$, $L'=\ln(1+a/(\lambda_A\mu_B))$; hence ${\rm Re}\,W_{12}=\frac12+O(\varepsilon L'^2)$. (b) *(model, proved)* ${\rm Im}W_{12}=0$ for configurations symmetric under $1\leftrightarrow2$. (c) *(model, proved: sensitivity)* with the even Lorentzian regulator $\chi_K(k^-)$ on the $A$-side minus components of both lines, $\lambda_A\ll K\ll a/\lambda_B$: $|W_{12}(K)-\frac12|\le C_e\big[\frac{K\lambda_B}{a_{\min}}(1+\ln(1+\frac{a_{\min}}{K\mu_B}))^2+\frac{\lambda_A}K(1+\ln(1+\frac{a_{\min}}{K\mu_B}))^2+\varepsilon(1+L')^2\big]$, every term boost invariant and $C_e$ an explicit polynomial in $m_A,m^\flat_A,m^\flat_B,a_{\max}/a_{\min}$: any in-window Lorentzian suppression of $|k^-|\gtrsim K$ removes the $O(1)$ part of ${\rm Im}W_{12}$ up to this bound (an instability under such regulators, not a statement that the $O(1)$ part is "not determined by the integrand on a window"). (d) *(numerical)* ${\rm Im}W_{12}(K)$ is linear in $K$ up to slowly varying factors (the logarithms of (c) are not sharp). The half-coincident part of `asm:appO-collinear-ends` is thus proved in the model with the $k^-$ regulator **alone**; adding the class-$\mathfrak X$ $k^+$ factor is expected to cost $O(a/(K^+K^-))$ (not proved). `\uses{def:appO-glob-model, def:appO-regulator-class, lem:appO-multipole-localization, lem:appO-kplus-rotation, lem:appO-two-insertion-bounds, lem:appO-halfcoinc-colour, asm:appO-adjacent-swap, asm:appO-real-jets, asm:appO-collinear-ends}`. Proof: (a) `lem:appO-kplus-rotation`, $A$-side localization and the $S$-type bounds of `lem:appO-two-insertion-bounds` for $f_B$; imaginarity by (REAL) and the oddness of $v$; (c) rotation to $\theta=\pi/4$ with the regulator, localization with `lem:appO-multipole-localization` ($\sum|w|\le2$, $c_U\le1+\sqrt2$), $|v_K|\le\min(\frac12,8KR/a_{\min})$, integration with `lem:appO-two-insertion-bounds`(h); (d) X1 `final_scripts/t3f_recompute.py`. **地位与缺口.** [P] as a QCD statement (model theorem): (a)–(c) are proved in the model; the QCD transfer needs (ADJ), (REAL) (\tagP entries) and the physical reading `asm:appO-collinear-ends` (open, together with the cut contributions); the fate of the colour factor for generic remnants is open (`lem:appO-halfcoinc-colour`). Ours (no novelty marker before the literature check).
- **注 `rem:appO-cut-ss` — 被割的直接 SS 线（实发射）** (no tag). In $\mathcal G_0$ a direct SS gluon may cross the final-state cut (emitted by spectator $i$ in the amplitude and absorbed by $j$ in the conjugate); `asm:appO-mixing`(c) includes these real-emission contributions; an on-shell gluon has $2k^+k^-=\mathbf k_T^2$, so they lie outside every window and are **not** part of the side sums of `prop:appO-twoexch-spectator`, `prop:appO-twoexch-regulated` and `prop:appO-halfcoinc-sensitivity`. Power counting in $\mathbb M_{\rm glob}$ (a careful estimate, not a proof): a single insertion alone on one side of a jet localizes (it contributes only through its remainder $O(M\lambda/q^2)$, since the on-shell condition forbids $q=0$), but two insertions on the same side of a jet do not, and the δ-like single insertions on the other jet then force $|k_1^-|\gtrsim a/\lambda_B$ — an $O(N_0)$ contribution at the collinear ends; so in the unregulated model the cut contributions of both coincident patterns are **not expected to be power suppressed**; with the two-sided regulators of `def:appO-regulator-class`(c) every cut configuration has a jet with a lone insertion far above its scale, which suggests $O(\lambda_BK^-/a+\lambda_AK^+/a)$ up to logarithms. Open (Ch25): compute them or prove the suppression; together with the three-gluon-vertex graphs this decides the full $\mathcal G_0$ form of the uncut-line result; the matching of `asm:appO-collinear-ends` must also absorb these collinear-end cut contributions.
- **命题 `prop:appO-g6-ladder` — $O(g^6)$：直接线梯形图类在幺正性下不封闭** [A]. Two on-shell eikonal (or massive) lines $A$, $B$; $\mathsf C$ = the class of direct-line ladder graphs at $O(g^6)$ (all three gluons join the two lines, any crossings); unitarity defect $D_{\mathsf C}={\rm Disc}_s(\sum_{\Gamma\in\mathsf C}\Gamma)-\int d\Phi_2(\mathcal M_{\mathsf C})^\dagger\mathcal M_{\mathsf C}$ at $O(g^6)$. (i) *(interference form)* by Cutkosky's rules graph by graph, $D_{\mathsf C}$ is the sum of the three-particle cuts of class-$\mathsf C$ graphs, which cut one rung whose ends lie on opposite sides of the cut: $D_{\mathsf C}=\int d\Phi_3[A_A^\dagger A_B+A_B^\dagger A_A]$, the interference of emission from line $A$ with emission from line $B$ (the self terms lie outside $\mathsf C$); $A_X$ is the tree $2\to3$ amplitude with one gluon exchanged between the lines and one radiated from line $X$ (emissions before and after the exchange combined), with exchanged-gluon propagators $1/\mathbf q_2^{\,2}$ and $1/\mathbf q_1^{\,2}$ ($q_1$, $q_2$ the momenta exchanged on either side of the emission). (ii) *(sign in Feynman gauge)* for straight lines the line parts of the Lipatov vertex give $A_A\propto C\,v_A^\mu/(v_A\cdot k)$, $A_B\propto-C\,v_B^\mu/(v_B\cdot k)$, $C=f^{a_1a_2b}T_A^{a_1}T_B^{a_2}$, and $D_{\mathsf C}\propto\int d\Phi_3\,\frac{2\,C^\dagger C\,(v_A\cdot v_B)^3}{(v_A\cdot k)(v_B\cdot k)\,q_1^2q_2^2}>0$; the self terms $-C^\dagger C\,v_X^2/(v_X\cdot k)^2\le0$ vanish for light-like lines. (iii) *(gauge dependence)* $\mathsf C$ is **not gauge invariant** without the central (three-gluon) term of the Lipatov vertex, so $D_{\mathsf C}$ is gauge dependent; the full $2\to3$ amplitude squared is gauge invariant (conserved total current). Conclusion: in Feynman gauge with eikonal lines $D_{\mathsf C}\ne0$ — the class is not closed under unitarity, and the gauge-invariant $2\to3$ amplitude needs the full Lipatov vertex; in Drell–Yan part of this real emission corresponds to cut direct lines (inside $\mathcal G_0$), part to soft lines emitted and absorbed by one spectator (outside $\mathcal G_0$). It concerns the closure of $\mathcal G_0$ under unitarity (the SS-family bookkeeping), not a failure of factorization; consistent with `Schwartz:2018obd` (pure Glauber ladders cancel for single-scale observables; the leading violation needs the Lipatov vertex) and `RothsteinStewart2016`. `\uses{prop:dy-eikonal-twogluon, lem:appO-glauber-operator}`. Proof plan: Cutkosky's rules (`Cutkosky1960`); the Lipatov line currents (`KuraevLipatovFadin1977`); numerical check of the interference identity, its Feynman-gauge sign and its gauge dependence (X1 `final_scripts/p5_interference.py`). **地位与缺口.** [A]: an argument with standard substance (BFKL); the interference identity and its sign are checked numerically for eikonal currents, not proven in general; the formulation (closure of the direct-line class) is ours. The fully coincident two-line ladder class from $O(g^6)$ on therefore needs `asm:appO-mixing`(c); expected, not shown, for the other coincident patterns.

### O.6 `sec:appO-rewriting` 单侧改写 (3.5 pp)

- **引理 `lem:appO-carrier` — 整函数载子** (数学). $\phi(x)=2\sqrt\pi\,e^{-x^2}$ is entire, $\int\frac{dx}{2\pi}\phi(x)=1$, $|\phi(x+iy)|\le2\sqrt\pi\,e^{y^2-x^2}$; hence for $|{\rm Im}\,k|\le\frac12w$: $|\phi(k/w)|\le2\sqrt\pi e^{1/4}$, the integral of $\phi(k/w)/w$ along any such contour equals $2\pi$, and the mass of $\phi(\cdot/w)/w$ beyond $|k|\ge b$ is $O(e^{-b^2/w^2})$. `% uses: none`. Proof: full. Check: R8 K7.
- **定义 `def:appO-weak-equivalence` — 测量相容的割图求和与弱等价** (no tag). (a) *Collinear DY:* $\mathcal C(\Gamma)$ = all final cuts through $\gamma^*$ with given $Q^2$, $y$ (spectators, unobserved jets, soft radiation and $\mathbf q_T$ integrated); $F_\Gamma\equiv\sum_{C\in\mathcal C(\Gamma)}\Gamma_C$ on the LCOPT domain (minus components of $J_A$'s internal loops and $\mathcal E$, plus components of $J_B$'s, integrated over $\mathbb R$; jet plus/transverse components and all components of uncut soft lines kept); $U_R$, $N_{R'}$ are their projections. (b) *TMD:* add $\delta^{(2)}(\mathbf q_T-\mathbf q_T(\gamma^*))$ (spectator transverse momenta stay integrated). (c) $\mathfrak B$ is the set algebra generated by the $N_{R'}(\lambda)$ (so $U_{R'}\in\mathfrak B$). $F\doteq_RF'$ iff there are $C,a>0,K$ such that for all $V\in\mathfrak B$, $V\subset U_R$, and all $\Phi\in\{1\}\cup\{C_{R'}:R'\in\mathcal R\}$ the integrals exist (each denominator with its own $i\eta_0\to0^+$, real space) and $|\int_V\Phi(F-F')\,d\mu|\le C\lambda^aL^K$ uniformly for $\lambda\in(0,\lambda_0]$, $\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_0]$ (error type E-W). Weak equivalence is the only possible form: $F_\Gamma$ carries pinched $i0$ distributions and is not $L^1$, so the integral norm of `def:method-power-suppressed` is undefined for it. `\uses{def:appO-leading-data, def:method-neighbourhoods, def:method-power-suppressed, def:method-subtraction}`.
- **定义 `def:appO-rewriting` — 改写映射 $\mathfrak r_R$** (no tag). Acting on the cut-summed integrand and on every chain term: **(R1)** $J_A$, $J_B$ in the closed-time-path form of `lem:appO-unitarity`; **(R2)** for each localizable unit $u$ a unit localization operator $\mathfrak l_u$: for a $J_A$ endpoint $j$ replace the jet factor $\mathcal J(k_j^-)$ by $\bar{\mathcal J}_j\,\phi(k_j^-/w_j)/w_j$ and set $k_j^-=0$ in the external factor, with $\bar{\mathcal J}_j=\mathcal J(0)$ (spectators), $\bar{\mathcal J}_j=c_j\mathcal J_+$, $c_j=R_{j\downarrow}(0)/R_j(0)$ (active lines emitted by $V_H$; $c_j=\theta(k_j^+)$ in $\mathcal G_0$), and, for coincident insertions on one parton and side, the jointly localized value $\sum_\pi w_\pi\mathcal J_\pi$ of `lem:appO-joint-localization`; $J_B$ likewise; SS lines: both ends; endpoints on active lines **absorbed** by $V_H$ unchanged. Then $\mathfrak r_RG\equiv\prod_u[\Theta_u\mathfrak l_u+(1-\Theta_u)]G$; $\tilde F_{\Gamma,R}\equiv\mathfrak r_RF_\Gamma$; subtraction and chain terms of rewritten forms are **defined** by $C_{R'}\tilde F\equiv\mathfrak r_RC_{R'}F_\Gamma$, $T_{R_0}X_{R_0}[\tilde F]\equiv\mathfrak r_RT_{R_0}X_{R_0}[F_\Gamma]$ ("first $T_c$, then rewrite"; $T_R$ is defined only on rational functions times measurement functions). `\uses{lem:appO-carrier, def:appO-windows, lem:appO-unitarity, lem:appO-insertion-values, lem:appO-joint-localization, def:method-subtraction}`.
- **假设 `asm:appO-envelope` — 纤维积分误差的可积包络** [\tagA]. Full text in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. Content: for each leading region $R$ there is $\hat\Phi_R\ge0$ on $U_R$, independent of the localized soft components, with $\int_{U_R}\hat\Phi_R\,d\hat\mu\le CL^K$ uniformly in $\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_0]$ and in the $i0$ regulator $\eta_0$, such that for $\Gamma$ and every chain term $T_c\Gamma$: (i) the fibre-integration error of each unit localization (`prop:appO-ss-localize`, the remainder of `lem:appO-equal-time`(iii), the remainder of `lem:appO-joint-localization`(b), truncation terms at fibre endpoints), with the other $i0$ replaced by $i\eta_0$, is bounded pointwise by $C\lambda^a\hat\Phi_R$; (ii) on boundary layers of relative width $\lambda^a$ (including fibre endpoints set by neighbourhoods $N_{R'}$ with $\beta_{R'}>\beta_R$), $\int\hat\Phi_R\le C\lambda^aL^K$. **For coincident connections the bounds are imposed on side-summed families** (rewording v2.3: within the class of `prop:appO-pergraph-weights` the per-graph version cannot hold; for graph-generated jets the per-graph question is open). `\uses{def:appO-rewriting, def:appO-windows}`.
- **假设 `asm:appO-mixing` — Glauber–软混合与重合连接的完整图集相消** [\tagA]. Full text in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. Content: $\Gamma$ a DY forward graph, $R$ a leading region, windows as in `def:appO-windows`, $\hat\Phi_R$ as in `asm:appO-envelope`. (a′) (*pre-integration localization with envelope bounds*; replaces the earlier half-plane splitting clause (a)) localization is performed before the integration over the internal loops of $S$, pointwise outside the exceptional sets $E_j$ of `lem:appO-preintegration-localization`; assumed: envelope bounds $C\lambda^aL^K$ (i) for the $S$-loop integral of the absolute localization error, including the factor $\sum_\sigma|w_\sigma(p)|$ when there are several insertions, and (ii) for the unlocalized integrand on the $E_j$ — these bounds also justify the Fubini interchange; same for $J_B$; (b) (*cut compatibility of $S$*) after the localization of (a′) the cut sum of $J_A\otimes J_B\otimes S$ takes the closed-time-path form of `lem:appO-unitarity` with $S$'s fields as $\langle0|\bar{\rm T}\{\cdots\}{\rm T}\{\cdots\}|0\rangle$, and the $\tau=0$ insertions with both ends on spectators (also through $S$) assemble into operators on $\mathcal H_A\otimes\mathcal H_B\otimes\mathcal H_S$; (c) (*complete graph set*) for the set $\mathfrak G$ of all forward graphs of the given order with the given $J_A$, $J_B$ (closed under adding soft three- and four-gluon vertices between Glauber and soft lines, including real soft emission through Lipatov-type vertices), summed over all coincident connections and side assignments, combining the window with the adjacent near-on-shell soft region of $U_R$ (including real emission), the contribution after $\mathfrak P$ is $O(\lambda^aL^K)$ in the weak sense; equivalently $\mathcal W'^\dagger\mathcal W=1+O(\lambda^a)$ on spectator states at $\tau=0$. The per-graph fixed-order clause of the earlier formulation is deleted (replaced by `lem:appO-joint-localization`). Status sentence (v2.3): (c) holds for two-gluon exchange between eikonal lines at $O(g^4)$ (`prop:dy-eikonal-twogluon`); for composite spectators in the global two-time model the uncut-line part of the fully coincident $O(g^4)$ family cancels pointwise on NF (`prop:appO-twoexch-spectator`, model theorem); the cut direct lines, the half-coincident pattern at the collinear ends, $O(g^6)$ (`prop:appO-g6-ladder`) and coincident SA/AS insertions are open. `\uses{def:appO-windows, def:appO-family, def:appO-weak-equivalence, asm:appO-envelope, lem:appO-unitarity, lem:appO-equal-time, lem:appO-preintegration-localization}`.
- **命题 `prop:appO-one-sided` — 割图求和后的单侧性（弱等价）** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appO-lcopt}、\cref{asm:appO-envelope}、\cref{asm:appN-envelope}、\cref{asm:appN-separation}（一般 $\Gamma$ 另需 \cref{asm:appO-mixing}(a′)(b)）。（本命题只涉及部分子态。）Let $R$ have no H-emission endpoints. For $(\Gamma,R)\in\mathcal G_0$ (general $\Gamma$ under `asm:appO-mixing`(a′)(b)), for all $V\in\mathfrak B$, $V\subset U_R$, and all chain terms $T_c$ (including $T_\varnothing=1$): $\int_V(T_cF_\Gamma-\mathfrak r_RT_cF_\Gamma)\,d\mu=O(\lambda^aL^K)$; in particular $F_\Gamma\doteq_R\tilde F_{\Gamma,R}$. In $\tilde F_{\Gamma,R}$ and every $\mathfrak r_RT_cF_\Gamma$: (a) where $\Theta_u=1$, each uncut soft $k_j^-$ appears in $J_A$ either only in initial denominators (endpoint on an active line absorbed by $V_H$) or in no denominator (only in $\phi$), never in cut $\delta$'s; likewise $k_j^+$ in $J_B$; in particular on $U_R\setminus K_R$; (b) on $U_R\setminus K_R$ no momentum is Glauber-pinched, and for any deformation with $|{\rm Im}\,k_j^-|\le\frac12w_j$ the carriers stay bounded and their integrals unchanged. `\uses{asm:appO-lcopt, asm:appO-envelope, asm:appN-envelope, asm:appN-separation, asm:appO-mixing, def:appO-rewriting, def:appO-weak-equivalence, lem:appO-unitarity, lem:appO-insertion-values, lem:appO-joint-localization, prop:appO-ss-localize, lem:appO-carrier, lem:appO-orientation}`. Proof: full given the premises — (R1) exact; (R2) telescoping one unit at a time; whole real axis: spectator endpoints in $\mathcal G_0$ have one external pole at distance $2\Lambda_j$ with $\nu_A/\Lambda_j\le\lambda^{a_G}$ at $\Theta_u>0$; emitted active lines give $c_jR(0)\mathcal J_+$; SS lines by `prop:appO-ss-localize`; coincident insertions by `lem:appO-joint-localization`(b); fibre vs real axis: tails $O(\nu_A/|b|)$ and carrier mass $O(e^{-b^2/w^2})$, boundary layers by `asm:appO-envelope`(ii); chain terms (step (O-4)): $T_c$ acts on $H$ (projections; merged with $V_H$), on small soft components ($k\to\pi_\mp(k)$, orientation of $p^2-M^2+2p^+k^-+i0$ kept because $p^+$ keeps its sign on $U_R$ by `asm:appN-separation`(ii)), on $S$ (kept or eikonalized) — none alters the ordering structure, (ET1)(ET2) are preserved with $\lambda_J$ not increased, windows are fixed functions; integrate with `asm:appO-envelope`(i); integral forms of the E-S approximations by `asm:appN-envelope`. Error type: **E-W**. Surpass: per-graph statement now also for coincident insertions (ERR-S3).
- **注 `rem:appO-weak` — 为何只有弱等价；SA/AS 线；窗口外** (no tag). (1) An equal-time insertion cannot be represented by a $k^-$ function with only initial denominators (upper-half-plane analytic with $k^{-2}$ decay integrates to 0; with $k^{-1}$ decay it is cutoff dependent) — hence the entire carrier. (2) SA lines: localized at the $A$ end ($k_j^-$ only in $\phi$), $k_j^+$ only in $J_B$'s initial denominators; §O.7 moves $k_j^-$ by $\le\frac12w_j$ and $k_j^+$ by $\sim\lambda^{-a_G}|\mathbf k_{Tj}|$; their allocation among subtraction terms (the $A$-end Glauber recoil is not reproduced by $T_R$) is part of `asm:appO-rewritten-cs`. (3) On $K_R$ (including the genuine soft scale $k\sim\lambda^2(1,1,1)$, where $\Lambda_j\sim\nu_A$ and the localization error is $O(1)$) nothing is rewritten and no contour is provided: `asm:appO-deformation`(c). (4) The final result is independent of the auxiliary choices $a_G,\delta,\kappa,\rho,\vartheta$ (the integral does not depend on them); only the error constants do.
- **注 `rem:appO-h-emission` — H-发射端点的障碍** (no tag). For localizable endpoints on active lines emitted by $V_H$ ($\tau>0$ only: $J=J^{\rm fi}$, $\mathcal J_-=0$), the localized value $c_j\mathcal J_+R_j(0)=\mathcal J_+R_{j\downarrow}(0)$ depends on the half-plane of the external pole; in $\mathcal G_0$, $c_j=\theta(k_j^+)$, non-analytic in the component seen by the other jet. Localizing the other end then meets a non-analytic external factor, and deforming $k_j^+$ is blocked; not localizing leaves $k_j^-$ final-state one-sided with a **future-pointing** eikonal factor whose combination with the Ward identity is not analysed. Such regions (they occur only if $H$ emits collinear lines into $J_A$ with uncut soft lines attached) are outside the proven scope: `asm:appO-deformation`(d). The same non-analyticity arises for coincident SA/AS insertions through the ordering weights of `lem:appO-joint-localization`(d) (ERR-S3 consequence). *Refinement (argument, ungraded):* for one SS and one SA line on one $A$-spectator the $A$-localized weights are continuous but not analytic at $k^+_{\rm SA}=0$ and depend on $k^+_{\rm SA}/k^+_{\rm SS}$, while the $k^+_{\rm SA}$ integral against $B$'s active-line factor is dominated by $|k^+_{\rm SA}|\sim\lambda_B$, where that factor is not eikonal: the uniform deformation that produces the past-pointing Wilson line needs analyticity across 0, and deforming each half line separately leaves a non-eikonal neighbourhood of 0 with $O(1)$ weight — the obstruction is the non-analyticity in the region that dominates the integral, not the discontinuity itself; for a non-coincident SA line the deformation works (open problem, Ch25).

### O.7 `sec:appO-deformation` 同时形变 (4.5 pp)

Glauber direction (recap, no environment; VF-REF-7, row 38): $e_G=n_B-n_A$, $(e_G^+,e_G^-,\mathbf e_{G,T})=(-1,+1,\mathbf 0_T)$, $e_G^2=-2$, $n_A\cdot e_G=+1$, $n_B\cdot e_G=-1$; $(l+it\sigma fe_G)^2-m^2=(l^2-m^2+2t^2f^2)+2it\sigma f(l^+-l^-)$: the deformation only pushes real parts towards time-like, so only space-like lines with $0<m^2-l^2\le2f^2$ are dangerous. Contrast (print): deforming only $k^-$ ($v^2=0$) gives ${\rm Im}\,k^2=2\kappa k^+$ at the on-shell point and forces $\kappa\sim\mathbf k_T^2/|k^+|$ (`lem:method-i0-compat`); along $e_G$ inside the window ($k^2\le-(1-2\delta)\mathbf k_T^2$) the amplitude $\kappa|\mathbf k_T|$ is allowed. Pointer: `fig:method-glauber-direction`, `rem:method-two-component` (Ch9).

- **定义 `def:appO-deformation-map` — 形变映射与 Glauber 流** (no tag). $0<\kappa\le\frac12$, $\rho$ = twice the soft-neighbourhood radius; switch $\psi(q)=\vartheta\big(\frac{|q^+q^-|}{\delta q_T^2}\big)\vartheta\big(\frac{\|q\|}{\rho}\big)$ ($\psi=0$ at $q_T=0$; $\psi=1$ on the core $\{|q^+q^-|\le\frac12\delta q_T^2,\ \|q\|\le\frac12\rho\}$; $q\mapsto\psi(q)|q_T|$ continuous, locally Lipschitz on $\{q_T\ne0\}$). (i) *Direct lines* ($k_j$ into $J_A$, side $\sigma_j$): $k_j^-\mapsto k_j^-+i\sigma_j\kappa\psi_As_A|\mathbf k_{Tj}|$, $k_j^+\mapsto k_j^+-i\sigma_j\kappa\psi_Bs_B|\mathbf k_{Tj}|$, $\mathbf k_{Tj}$ fixed; AA: $\psi_A=\psi_B=\psi(k_j)$, $s_A=s_B=1$ (i.e. $k_j\mapsto k_j+i\sigma_j\kappa\psi|\mathbf k_{Tj}|e_G$); SA (A end localizable): $\psi_A=\psi\Theta'_j$, $s_A=\lambda^{a_G}$, $\psi_B=\psi$, $s_B=1+(\lambda^{-a_G}-1)\Theta'_j$ — at $\Theta'_j=1$, $k_j^-$ moves by $\kappa w_j\le\frac12w_j$ and $k_j^+$ by $\kappa\lambda^{-a_G}|\mathbf k_{Tj}|$; for $0<\Theta'_j<1$, $k_j^-$ moves by $\kappa\psi\Theta'_jw_j$ (corrected statement; fixed only where $\Theta'_j=0$); AS by $A\leftrightarrow B$; SS: not deformed; H-emission endpoints: not defined (`rem:appO-h-emission`). (ii) *Glauber flow* on a $\sigma$-side uncut soft component $C$ with both $A$- and $B$-legs and no localized legs: real $f_l$ on every line (incl. legs) with conservation at internal vertices of $C$, $f=0$ on cut lines, $f_a\ge\kappa\psi(q_a)|q_{Ta}|$ on $A$-legs, $-f_b\ge\kappa\psi(q_b)|q_{Tb}|$ on $B$-legs; deformation $l\mapsto l+it\sigma f_le_G$, $t\in[0,1]$, realized by loop-momentum deformations closed through $J_A\to H\to J_B$; $\Omega_t$, $\Omega\equiv\Omega_1$. Not covered: components with localized legs and internal vertices; components with only one kind of legs (`asm:appO-deformation`(a)). Optional figure `fig:appO-glauber-flow`. `\uses{def:appO-windows, def:appO-leading-data, def:method-neighbourhoods}`.
- **引理 `lem:appO-no-crossing` — 形变不跨越奇点** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）$0<\kappa\le\frac12$, $0<\delta\le\frac18$. Let $F$ be any integrand with the structure of the rewritten forms: (F1) $J_A$ depends on soft momenta only through initial denominators $D=\mathcal A_D+\sum_{a\in I(D)}q_a^-+i\sigma0$ and polynomial numerators, localized $A$-legs only through $\phi(q_a^-/w_a)$ ($J_B$ alike); (F2) $H$, $H^\dagger$ depend only on $l_A^+,l_B^-,q$ and the measurement only on $q$ (TMD: transverse momenta); (F3) uncut soft lines carry exact propagators, localized units as in `def:appO-rewriting`. Then for all $t\in[0,1]$ on $\Omega_t$: (a) every $J_A$, $J_B$ energy denominator obeys $\sigma{\rm Im}\,D\ge\sigma{\rm Im}\,D|_{t=0}>0$; (b) every deformed direct line with $\psi>0$ has ${\rm Re}(k_t^2)\le-\frac14\mathbf k_T^2$; carriers on localized legs have $|{\rm Im}\,k_j^\mp|\le\frac12w_j$, hence $|\phi|\le2\sqrt\pi e^{1/4}$; (c) every other uncut soft line $l$ with non-zero flow is non-singular provided at the real point $(N_l)$: $l^2-m_l^2\ge0$ or $m_l^2-l^2>2f_l^2$; (d) $H$, collinear lines without soft momentum, cut $\delta$'s and the measurement are unaffected; (e) approximator-generated denominators of $C_{R'}F$ are non-zero **provided the eikonal denominators take the past-pointing prescription**: soft-approximated jet denominators keep the form of (F1), and eikonal denominators $n_A\cdot K+i\sigma0$, $n_B\cdot K'+i\sigma0$ (or with $n_A(y_A)$, $n_B(y_B)$ of `def:appN-tmd-approx`) satisfy $\sigma{\rm Im}\ge0$, since ${\rm Im}(n_A\cdot K)=t\sigma\sum f_a$, ${\rm Im}(n_B\cdot K')=-t\sigma\sum f_b$, $n_A(y_A)\cdot e_G=1+e^{-2y_A}>0$, $n_B(y_B)\cdot e_G=-(1+e^{2y_B})<0$. Hence if every flow-carrying soft internal line satisfies $(N_l)$ (vacuous in $\mathcal G_0$), $\int_{\mathbb R}F=\int_\Omega F$ (deform at $i\eta_0$, then $\eta_0\to0$). `\uses{def:appO-deformation-map, def:appO-rewriting, lem:appO-carrier, lem:appO-orientation, def:appN-tmd-approx}`. Proof: full — (a) ${\rm Im}\,q_a^-=t\sigma f_a$; a final denominator would give $\eta_0-t\sum f_a$ and cross zero (the single-cut-graph pinch); (b) ${\rm Re}(k_t^2)=k^2+2t^2\kappa^2\psi_A\psi_Bs_As_B\mathbf k_T^2\le(2\delta-1+2\kappa^2)\mathbf k_T^2\le-\frac14\mathbf k_T^2$ (scan: maximum $-0.388\,\mathbf k_T^2$, R8 K9); (c) from the deformed-denominator formula; (e) σ restored (ERR-m27); Stokes with boundary faces $|\mathbf k_{Tj}|=\gamma\to0$. Error type: exact.
- **定义 `def:appO-graded` — 分级构型** (no tag). $C$ a two-leg-type component with $N_C$ legs, $E$ an open set of real soft momenta. $C$ is *graded on $E$* (constant $K\ge1$) if there are positive functions $s_1\ge\dots\ge s_m$ on $E$ and levels $i(l)$ with **(GC1)** $\|l\|\le s_{i(l)}$; **(GC2)** $l$ space-like ⇒ $m_l^2-l^2\ge s_{i(l)}^2/K$; **(GC3)** each leg $e$ with $\psi(q_e)>0$ has a path $\pi_e$ in $C$ to a leg of the other jet through lines with $i(l)\le i(e)$. ($m=1$: single soft scale; $s_{i+1}\le\theta s_i$: strong ordering.) Renamed items: the reference's (S1)–(S3) are (GC1)–(GC3). `\uses{def:appO-deformation-map}`.
- **定义 `def:appO-target` — 形变目标与相容性** (no tag). $G$ an integrand (e.g. $\tilde F_{\Gamma,R}$); $h:[0,1]\times\mathbb R^N\to\mathbb C^N$ continuous, $h_0={\rm id}$, moving only the $\pm$ components of $R$'s uncut soft loop momenta; $\Omega=h_1(\mathbb R^N)$ is a *deformation target* for $G$ if (a) $h_t(x)=x$ for large $\|k\|$ or when every soft momentum of $R$ is outside a fixed neighbourhood of $G_\delta$; (b) on $h_1(U_R\setminus K_R)$ every uncut soft momentum appearing in a denominator of $G$ has $|k_j^+||k_j^-|\ge c\,\mathbf k_{Tj}^2$ ($c$ independent of $\lambda$); (c) for all $t$, $h_t(\mathbb R^N)$ contains no singularity of $G$ or of $C_{R'}G$. It is *compatible with $\mathfrak B$* if moreover (d) $\int_VG'd\mu=\int_{h_1(V)}G'd\mu_\Omega$ for all $V\in\mathfrak B$, $V\subset U_R$, $G'\in\{G\}\cup\{C_{R'}G\}$. (a)–(c) are the Glauber part of (GA1)(a) and of the first part of (GA4)(iii) (`asm:appN-contour`, `asm:appN-separation`); a sufficient condition for (d): each moved component's fibre of $V$ is a finite union of intervals at whose endpoints $h_t$ is the identity; general $V$ fail it. `\uses{def:appO-windows, def:appO-weak-equivalence, def:appO-pinch, def:method-subtraction}`.
- **命题 `prop:appO-target` — $\mathcal G_0$ 与分级构型的形变目标** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本命题只涉及部分子态。）$0<\delta\le\frac18$, $0<\kappa\le\frac12$. (i) For $\Gamma\in\mathcal G_0$ without H-emission endpoints, $\Omega$ of `def:appO-deformation-map`(i) is a deformation target ((a)–(c) of `def:appO-target`); on $U_R\setminus K_R$ at $\|k\|\le\rho/2$ every deformed soft momentum satisfies $|k^+||k^-|\ge c\,\mathbf k_T^2$ with $c=\min(\kappa^2,\delta/2)$; (ii) for general $\Gamma$: if every two-leg-type component without localized legs is graded on $E_C$ and $\kappa^2<1/(2KN_C^2)$, there is a Glauber flow such that every line satisfies $(N_l)$ on $E_C$ and every leg $|q_e^+||q_e^-|\ge cq_{Te}^2$; (iii) gluing over a finite cover $\{E_\alpha\}$ by a partition of unity for $\kappa^2<\min_\alpha1/(2K_\alpha N_C^2)$, provided components with localized legs and internal vertices and single-leg-type components are absent. `\uses{lem:appO-no-crossing, def:appO-graded, def:appO-target, def:appO-deformation-map}`. Proof: full — (i) independent loop momenta for direct lines; if $|k^+k^-|\le\frac12\delta\mathbf k_T^2$ then $\psi=1$ and $|k^+||k^-|\ge|{\rm Im}\,k^+||{\rm Im}\,k^-|=\kappa^2\mathbf k_T^2$, else $\ge|{\rm Re}\,k^+||{\rm Re}\,k^-|>\frac12\delta\mathbf k_T^2$ (scan: minimum ratio 1.0006, R8 K10); (ii) routing $f_l=\sum_{e:l\in\pi_e}\pm\kappa\psi(q_e)|q_{Te}|$, $|f_l|\le\kappa N_Cs_{i(l)}$, space-like $l$: $m^2-l^2\ge s^2/K>2\kappa^2N_C^2s^2\ge2f_l^2$; (iii) the conditions are convex in $f$ at fixed real points. Error type: exact (geometry). Content new relative to the literature at this level of precision (not marked; constants explicit).
- **假设 `asm:appO-deformation` — 一般构型、相容性、软角区与 H-发射端点** [\tagA]. Full text in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. Content: for any $\Gamma$, $R$, $F=\tilde F_{\Gamma,R}$: (a) a deformation target exists (Glauber flows; two separately conserved flows on localized legs with imaginary parts $\le$ half the carrier width; internal deformations of $S$; single-leg-type components moved in the component their jet does not see) with the sign condition of `lem:appO-no-crossing`(a), $|q_e^+||q_e^-|\ge cq_{Te}^2$, and no denominator of $S$ crossed; (b) the target is compatible with $\mathfrak B$ (`def:appO-target`(d)); (c) on the soft-angle region — including the out-of-window parts of the side-independent windows and the wee-line endpoint region of `lem:appO-side-independent-windows`(c); the window-edge terms themselves vanish identically —, (CS1)$_R$ and (CS2)$_R$ hold for the rewritten forms: $\int_{h_1(U_R)}(1-\prod_u\Theta'_u)\big(|\mathfrak r_R(1-T_R)X_R[F_\Gamma]|+\sum_{R'\not\le R}|\mathfrak r_RC_{R'}F_\Gamma|\big)d\mu_\Omega\le C\lambda^aL^K$; (d) for regions with H-emission endpoints **and for coincident SA/AS insertions** (non-analytic ordering weights), a weakly equivalent rewriting exists in which each uncut soft momentum appears in each jet only in denominators of one orientation or only in carriers, with a deformation target such that `prop:appO-eikonal` and `prop:appO-past-wilson` hold. (a) is proved for $\mathcal G_0$ and graded configurations (`prop:appO-target`); **(b)(c)(d) are needed even for abelian $\mathcal G_0$**. `\uses{def:appO-target, def:appO-rewriting, def:appO-graded, prop:appO-ss-cancel, lem:appO-side-independent-windows}`.
- **命题 `prop:appO-eikonal` — 变形围道上的程函近似** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本命题只涉及部分子态。）Let $p$ be the $A$-collinear part of an initial-state $J_A$ line ($p^+>0$), $k=\sum_{a\in I}q_a$ the legs injected before it, all in the *Glauber core* ($\psi(q_a)=1$, $|{\rm Re}\,q_a^\pm|\le|q_{Ta}|$), $f_I=\sum_If_a\le\kappa N_Cs$. On the amplitude side of $\Omega$: (a) $|k^-|\ge{\rm Im}\,k^-=f_I\ge\kappa\sum_I|q_{Ta}|\ge\kappa|\mathbf k_T|$, so $|2p^+k^-|\ge2\kappa p^+|\mathbf k_T|$; (b) $\tilde D=p^2-m^2+2p^+k^-+i0$ has $|\tilde D|\ge2p^+f_I$ and $D=(p+k)^2-m^2+i0$ satisfies $\frac{|D-\tilde D|}{|\tilde D|}\le\frac{1}{\kappa p^+}\big(2|p^-|+|\mathbf p_T|+\frac92N_Cs\big)$; (c) with $\varepsilon_3\equiv(|k^+||p^-|+|\mathbf k_T||\mathbf p_T|)/(|k^-|p^+)\le\frac12$, the Grammer–Yennie ratio $G/K$ (`GrammerYennie1973`) is at most twice $r_+\frac{|p^-|}{p^+}+r_T\frac{|\mathbf p_T|}{p^+}+\frac{2|p^-|+|\mathbf p_T|}{\kappa p^+}$, $r_+=|S^+|/|S^-|$, $r_T=|\mathbf S_T|/|S^-|$ ($O(1)$); on the real contour in the Glauber region the last term is $O(1)$ (failure of the soft approximation), on $\Omega$ it is $O(\lambda/\kappa)$; (d) the eikonal denominators $n_A\cdot K+i\sigma0$ satisfy $\sigma{\rm Im}(n_A\cdot K)\ge0$ on $\Omega$; the unique compatible prescription is $+i\sigma0$, the past-pointing Wilson line $W[0,-\infty n_A]$ (VF-WL-1; = `lem:method-i0-compat` with $\eta=-1$). On $U_R$ the bounds are $O(\lambda^a/\kappa)$. Both components must be deformed (moving only $k^+$ leaves $k$ in $G_\delta$). `\uses{def:appO-deformation-map, prop:appO-target, lem:appO-no-crossing, lem:method-i0-compat}`. Proof: full ($|{\rm Re}\,k^\pm|\le f_I/\kappa$, $|k^\pm|\le2f_I/\kappa$, $|\mathbf k_T|\le f_I/\kappa$; $|D-\tilde D|=|2p^-k^+-2\mathbf p_T\cdot\mathbf k_T+2k^+k^--\mathbf k_T^2|$; $K^{\mu\nu}=n_A^\mu k^\nu/n_A\cdot k$). Check: random sampling (R8 K11; maximal ratios 0.77, 0.51, 0.89). Error type: E-P (on the deformed contour); off-core momenta controlled by the second part of (GA4)(iii) as in $e^+e^-$.
- Figure `fig:appO-deformation` (FIGURES_INDEX_v2 N22): three panels — $k^-$ plane ($J_A$: initial poles below, contour raised to ${\rm Im}\,k^-=\kappa|\mathbf k_T|$ over the window, the soft line's own pole outside the window), $k^+$ plane ($J_B$: lowered), real $(k^+,k^-)$ plane with the cross-shaped window bounded by $|k^+k^-|=\delta\mathbf k_T^2$ and the mass shell outside.

### O.8 `sec:appO-wilson` Wilson 线 (2.5 pp)

- **假设 `asm:appO-rewritten-cs` — 改写形式与族求和形式的 Collins 条件** [\tagA]. Full text in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. Content: (CS1)$_R$ and (CS2)$_R$ of `def:method-collins-conditions` hold for the rewritten forms $\mathfrak r_R(\cdot)$ and for the family-summed forms $\sum_{\mathfrak F}\mathfrak P[\cdot]$ on $h_1^{(R)}(U_R)$, including (i) the term-by-term matching of the localized spectator terms (plus the $(1-T_R)$ errors) with the external-endpoint distributions at $n_A\cdot K_i=0$ that fixes the Wilson-line prescription, and (ii) the allocation of localized SA/AS endpoints between the region $R$ and the $A$-collinear region $R'$ (the $A$-end Glauber recoil is not reproduced by $T_R$; overlap = zero-bin). Glauber specific (absent in DIS and $e^+e^-$); harmless for collinear PDFs (direction irrelevant), substantive for TMD. `\uses{def:method-collins-conditions, def:appO-rewriting, def:appO-family}`.
- **命题 `prop:appO-past-wilson` — Ward 恒等式与指向过去的 Wilson 线** [A]. Under `prop:appO-eikonal`, on $\Omega=\Omega_R$, summing all graphs and all attachments of soft gluons and longitudinal collinear gluons: (a) for the soft-approximated cut-summed integrand (the soft-factorized part of subtraction terms), the sum of all soft attachments to $J_A$ (including final-state spectators and lines emitted by $V_H$) equals, where the eikonal denominators are non-zero, $J_A$ without soft attachments times a Wilson line along $n_A$ ending at $V_H$; with the prescription fixed by the deformation it is the past-pointing $W[0,-\infty n_A]$, eikonal factors $\prod_i\frac{i}{n_A\cdot K_i+i0}$ (conjugate: Hermitian conjugate at $V_H^\dagger$); soft attachments to $J_B$ give $W[0,-\infty n_B]$; $A$-collinear longitudinal gluons attached to $H$ or $J_B$ give the past-pointing line $W[\cdot,-\infty n_B]$ in $A$'s collinear factor (direction irrelevant for collinear PDFs, `prop:method-collinear-wilson`); (b) the soft factor is $S^{(-)}(k_{\rm tot})=\sum_{X_s}\langle0|\bar{\rm T}[\mathcal U^{(-)\dagger}]|X_s\rangle\langle X_s|{\rm T}[\mathcal U^{(-)}]|0\rangle\delta^4(k_{\rm tot}-p_{X_s})$, $\mathcal U^{(-)}=W[0,-\infty n_A]\,W[0,-\infty n_B]$ (colour charges in the representations of the active partons); (c) localized SS endpoints cancel in the family sum (`prop:appO-ss-cancel`); SA endpoints belong, in the $A$-collinear region, to $J_A$ and become part of $A$'s past-pointing line (the $i0$ comes from $J_B$'s initial denominator); the term-by-term agreement on $U_R$ is `asm:appO-rewritten-cs`. `\uses{prop:appO-eikonal, prop:appO-ss-cancel, asm:appO-rewritten-cs, asm:appN-ward-product, lem:method-ward-multi, prop:method-soft-wilson, prop:method-collinear-wilson, prop:prelim-wilson-rules, lem:appO-no-crossing}`. Proof: sketch — (1) the soft approximation inserts $n_A^\mu n_{B\nu}=n_A^\mu\pi_-(k)_\nu/n_A\cdot k$ (the Grammer–Yennie K term); the Ward identities (`lem:method-ward-multi`, (WI1)) give endpoint terms; external endpoints vanish by LSZ; the $V_H$ endpoint term is the expansion of the Wilson line (`prop:prelim-wilson-rules`); (2) prescription: in a single cut graph the attachment sum equals the **future**-pointing line (`ex:dy-future-vs-past`: $\frac{iQ_a}{k^-+i0}+iQ_s[\frac1{k^-+i0}-\frac1{k^--i0}]=\frac{iQ_a}{k^--i0}$ for $Q_s=-Q_a$); after the rewriting, spectator attachments are localized at $\tau=0$ and attachments on absorbed active lines are initial-only, and on $\Omega$, $\sigma{\rm Im}(n_A\cdot K_i)>0$: moving back to the real contour without crossing poles needs $+i\sigma0$ (`lem:appO-no-crossing`(e)); (3) the Ward identity acts on unrewritten integrands and does not commute with $\mathfrak r_R$; the reconciliation is `asm:appO-rewritten-cs`(i). 「地位与缺口」: [A] because of step (3); harmless for collinear factorization (soft-factor unitarity holds for past, future or mixed directions), substantive for TMD (staple direction; Sivers sign). VF-REF-11 (single cut graph = future-pointing line, step 2); VF-WL-1 (eikonal rules); directions as in §00.5 of this file. Literature: `Collins2002`, `Collins2011` §14.4, `CollinsSoperSterman1988`.
- **注 `rem:appO-timelike` — 类时与类空的非光锥 Wilson 线** (no tag). The identification of path order with time order needs $v^0>0$ along the line towards $-\infty$; for time-like non-light-like past-pointing lines used as rapidity regulators path order is anti-time order and the analogue of `lem:method-soft-unitarity` fails at finite rapidity (c_tmd §3.4); App O and App N use Collins' space-like lines (`def:appN-tmd-approx`), for which the TMD statements below hold. Active–active Glauber exchange is the Coulomb phase of the soft factor (it makes the DY and SIDIS soft factors differ only by the direction of the lines, `RothsteinStewart2016` §10.1).
- *Target:* **`prop:dy-eikonal-twogluon`** (Ch12, [R, R_PT]) — 两条程函线之间的双胶子交换：$O(g^4)$ 幺正性与 Regge 相位. Setting: two infinite time-like Wilson lines $v_A$, $v_B$ (rapidity difference $Y>0$, colour generators $\mathbf T_A$, $\mathbf T_B$) exchange two gluons of mass $m$ (IR regulator) with exact propagators ($k^+k^-$ kept), at fixed transverse momenta; $a=\mathbf k_T^2+m^2$, $b=(\mathbf K-\mathbf k)_T^2+m^2$. (a) After the $\delta(v_A\cdot K)\delta(v_B\cdot K)$ of the infinite lines, with $x_A=v_A\cdot k$, $x_B=v_B\cdot k$, measure $dk^0dk^3=dx_Adx_B/\sinh Y$, $Q\equiv k_\parallel^2=(-(x_A^2+x_B^2)+2\cosh Y\,x_Ax_B)/\sinh^2Y$, $g=1/((Q-a+i0)(Q-b+i0))$: $J_{\rm box}=\int dx_Adx_B\frac{i}{x_A+i0}\frac{i}{-x_B+i0}g$, $J_{\rm cross}=\int dx_Adx_B\frac{i}{x_A+i0}\frac{i}{x_B+i0}g$ equal $J_{\rm box}=\frac{2\pi}{ab}(\pi+iY)$, $J_{\rm cross}=-\frac{2\pi iY}{ab}$; with $c_{\rm box}=X^2$, $c_{\rm cross}=X^2+\frac{C_A}{2}X$, $J_{\rm box}c_{\rm box}+J_{\rm cross}c_{\rm cross}=\frac{2\pi}{ab}[\pi X^2-iY\frac{C_A}{2}X]$; (b) after the transverse integrals the first term is the exponentiation term $\frac12(-i\chi)^2X^2$, the second is $i\times$real$\times$Hermitian — the one-loop reggeization phase $\propto C_AY$; (c) in the parametrization of `lem:appO-glauber-operator`, $p_{\rm opp}=-iY/\pi$, ${\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp}=0$, so the strict-window residual vanishes and the two-gluon exchange graphs give $\mathcal V^\dagger\mathcal V=1+O(\chi^3)$ with no real emission (for straight infinite lines real cuts start at $O(g^6)$); including the other $O(g^4)$ eikonal graphs (`Meggiolaro:1996hf` eqs. (3.28)–(3.34): a real multiple of the tree, with $F^{(2)}(t)$ real for $t<0$), unitarity holds at $O(g^4)$ for the complete eikonal amplitude; not shown here: non-eikonal spectators (model results: `prop:appO-twoexch-spectator`) and all orders (slot X1a not delivered; `rem:dy-common-ordering`(ii)). *Proof:* full — $x_B$ integral by residues: for $J_{\rm cross}$ close in the upper half-plane (poles $x_+^{a}$, $x_+^{b}$), ${\rm inner}_{\rm cross}(x_A)=-\frac{i\pi\sinh Y}{a-b}\big[\frac{1}{R_ax_+^a}-\frac{1}{R_bx_+^b}\big]$; for $J_{\rm box}$ close in the lower half-plane (poles $x_-^a$, $x_-^b$), ${\rm inner}_{\rm box}=\frac{i\pi\sinh Y}{a-b}\big[\frac{1}{R_ax_-^a}-\frac{1}{R_bx_-^b}\big]$, with $R_c=\sqrt{x_A^2-c+i0}$ (${\rm Im}\,R_c\ge0$), $x_\pm^c=\cosh Y\,x_A\pm\sinh Y\,R_c$; then $J=-\int dx_A\frac{{\rm inner}(x_A)}{x_A+i0}=-\big[{\rm PV}\!\int\frac{{\rm inner}}{x_A}-i\pi\,{\rm inner}(0)\big]$, evaluated in closed form. Checks: `refdoc/work_R3/scripts/eikonal_box.py`, `eikonal_box_check.py` (residue formulas vs brute-force $i\varepsilon$ quadrature), `eikonal_box_closedform.py` (closed form to $<5\times10^{-16}$ at 10 points). VF-REF-9, VF-REF-10. No novelty marker (claim N01 verified: the result is in `Meggiolaro:1996hf` §3, eqs. (3.23)–(3.26), (3.34), (3.36), and graph by graph in `Giordano:2006vv` eq. (4.24) — cite these; writer note: do not quote `Meggiolaro:1996hf` eq. (3.35) or `Giordano:2006vv` eqs. (4.15)–(4.21), (4.27), whose signs differ from the cited equations (not an App Q item); the high-energy limit is classic: `Cheng:1969eh`, `Korchemskaya:1994qp`, `Korchemsky:1993hr`, `Caron-Huot:2013fea`). Figure `fig:dy-two-gluon-exchange` (owner Ch12).
- **注 `rem:appO-onshell-unitarity` — 在壳线的两粒子幺正性（$O(g^4)$）** (no tag). For two on-shell external lines (massive scalars or time-like eikonal lines) with exact gluon propagators and a gluon-mass regulator, box plus crossed box obey exact two-particle unitarity at $O(g^4)$, ${\rm Disc}_s({\rm box}+{\rm crossed})=\int d\Phi_2\,T_1^\dagger T_1$, by Cutkosky's rules graph by graph (`Cutkosky1960`, `Veltman1963`, `tHooftVeltman1974`): the crossed box has no $s$-channel cut (it would need an on-shell emission from an on-shell massive line), three-particle cuts appear only at $O(g^6)$, and in the high-energy limit $p_{\rm opp}$ is purely imaginary (`prop:dy-eikonal-twogluon`) — the strict-window residual is not a property of the exact graphs (known in substance). For three on-shell lines $(i;j_1;j_2)$ the analogous relation suggests $W_{12}+W_{21}^*=1$, i.e. no half-coincident residual, but a complete argument must include the graphs that combine an $(i$–$j_e)$ exchange with a $(j_1$–$j_2)$ exchange (same colour structure) — open, not certified.

### O.9 `sec:appO-main` 主定理 (2 pp)

- **定理 `thm:appO-main` — 色单态单举 DY 中 Glauber 区相消的条件定理** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appO-lcopt}、\cref{asm:appO-envelope}、\cref{asm:appO-mixing}、\cref{asm:appO-deformation}、\cref{asm:appO-rewritten-cs}、\cref{asm:appN-envelope}、\cref{asm:appN-separation}、\cref{asm:appN-ward-product}（及 \cref{prop:appO-past-wilson}、\cref{prop:method-collinear-wilson}）。（本定理只涉及部分子态。）Setting: colour-singlet inclusive DY ($A$, $B$ colour-singlet partonic models; massless active partons), any order of non-abelian perturbation theory, summed over all cuts $\mathcal C(\Gamma)$ and all graphs of the order; $R$ a leading region, $U_R$ its dominated set on the LCOPT domain, $K_R$ the soft-angle region. Then: (i) (*rewrite*) $F_\Gamma\doteq_R\tilde F_{\Gamma,R}$, i.e. $\int_V(T_cF_\Gamma-\mathfrak r_RT_cF_\Gamma)=O(\lambda^aL^K)$ for all $V\in\mathfrak B$, $V\subset U_R$, and all chain terms (E-W); (ii) (*one-sidedness*) on $U_R\setminus K_R$, under standard flow, each uncut soft $k_j$ through $J_A$ ($J_B$) has $k_j^-$ ($k_j^+$) only in initial denominators or only in an entire carrier, never in a cut $\delta$; (iii) (*deformation and factorized structure*) a deformation target $\Omega_R$ exists (`prop:appO-target` for $\mathcal G_0$ and graded configurations, `asm:appO-deformation`(a) otherwise); on it the soft approximation for Glauber-core couplings to active lines holds with relative error $O(\lambda^a)$ (E-P); after summing graphs and attachments the approximated couplings become past-pointing Wilson lines — soft attachments to $J_A$ ($J_B$) give $W[0,-\infty n_A]$ ($W[0,-\infty n_B]$) in the soft factor, $A$-collinear ($B$-collinear) longitudinal gluons give $W[\cdot,-\infty n_B]$ ($W[\cdot,-\infty n_A]$) in the collinear factor of $A$ ($B$); the soft factor equals 1 and the collinear factors are the DIS PDFs; (iv) (*spectator exchange*) summed over a spectator-exchange family, the in-window SS exchanges of non-coincident connections cancel (all connections in the abelian theory); coincident connections (non-abelian) cancel only in the complete graph set (`asm:appO-mixing`(c)) — model evidence for the uncut-line part of the coincident $O(g^4)$ patterns: `prop:appO-twoexch-spectator`, `prop:appO-halfcoinc-sensitivity` (not used here); (v) the subtraction terms have the same structure (all statements hold for every $\mathfrak r_RT_cF_\Gamma$). Regions with H-emission endpoints: the corresponding parts of (i)–(iii) are replaced by `asm:appO-deformation`(d). `\uses{asm:appO-lcopt, asm:appO-envelope, asm:appO-mixing, asm:appO-deformation, asm:appO-rewritten-cs, asm:appN-envelope, asm:appN-separation, asm:appN-ward-product, prop:appO-past-wilson, lem:appO-hard-locality, lem:appO-orientation, lem:appO-unitarity, prop:appO-one-sided, prop:appO-ss-cancel, prop:appO-target, prop:appO-eikonal, lem:method-soft-unitarity, prop:method-collinear-wilson}`. Proof: full, eight steps (O-1)–(O-8) as in §O.1 with their error types; special cases: $\mathcal G_0$ without H-emission needs `asm:appO-mixing` only for $\ge2$ non-commuting localized insertions on one spectator parton; (O-5) needs no `asm:appO-deformation`(a) for $\mathcal G_0$/graded; (O-8) always needs (b)(c). Content grade [A]. **Remark to print:** `asm:appO-deformation`(b)(c)(d) are needed **even for abelian graphs** — no part of the DY Glauber cancellation is unconditional at the cross-section level. It supplies (CU1) of `asm:appN-cutsum` for these graphs (via `lem:appO-lcopt`, `lem:appO-hard-locality`).
- **推论 `cor:appO-interface` — 与附录 N 的接口** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appO-lcopt}、\cref{asm:appO-envelope}、\cref{asm:appO-mixing}、\cref{asm:appO-deformation}、\cref{asm:appO-rewritten-cs}、\cref{asm:appN-envelope}、\cref{asm:appN-separation}、\cref{asm:appN-ward-product}（及 \cref{prop:appO-past-wilson}）。（本推论只涉及部分子态。）With $F=F_\Gamma$, $\tilde F_{R_0}=\mathfrak r_{R_0}F$, $\Omega_{R_0}$, $h^{(R_0)}$ from `thm:appO-main`: **(Gla-a)** $F\doteq_{R_0}\tilde F_{R_0}$ for every $R_0$ (same $\mathfrak B$, all $\Phi$, uniform error); **(Gla-b)** on $U_{R_0}\setminus K_{R_0}$, in $\tilde F_{R_0}$ and each chain term, uncut soft momenta through $J_A$ ($J_B$) occur only with initial orientation or only in carriers, so `lem:method-i0-compat` applies with $\eta=-1$ to each jet denominator; **(Gla-c)** (GA1)(a) holds for $h^{(R_0)}$ and $\tilde F_{R_0}$ with its subtraction terms, and the first part of (GA4)(iii) on $h_1^{(R_0)}(U_{R_0}\setminus K_{R_0})$ for soft momenta in denominators; (d) (*transfer*) if for every $\Phi\in\{1\}\cup\{C_R\}$, $\int\Phi F$ converges absolutely on real space for each $\eta_0>0$ with a limit as $\eta_0\to0^+$, and (CS1)$_{R_0}$, (CS2)$_{R_0}$ hold for $\tilde F_{R_0}$ on $h_1^{(R_0)}(U_{R_0})$ (`asm:appO-rewritten-cs`, with `asm:appO-deformation`(b)(c)), then $|\int F-\sum_R\int C_RF|\le C\lambda^aL^K$; (e) (*block-wise subtraction terms*) for every $R$, $V\subset U_R$ in $\mathfrak B$, and $R'$: $\int_VC_{R'}F=\int_V\mathfrak r_RC_{R'}F+O(\lambda^aL^K)$ — on each $R$-dominated block the subtraction terms may be computed in rewritten form, with the Wilson-line prescription fixed by $\Omega_R$ (past pointing). `\uses{thm:appO-main, asm:appO-lcopt, asm:appO-envelope, asm:appO-mixing, asm:appO-deformation, asm:appO-rewritten-cs, asm:appN-envelope, asm:appN-separation, asm:appN-ward-product, prop:appO-past-wilson, lem:method-i0-compat, lem:method-covering, def:appO-target}`. Proof: full (elementary; (d) disjoint blocks $V_{R^{(m)}}=U_{R^{(m)}}\setminus\bigcup_{m'<m}U_{R^{(m')}}$ cover the space, `lem:method-covering`; on each block replace $F$ by $\tilde F_{R_0}$, move to $h_1(V_{R_0})$ by compatibility, bound pointwise by (CS1)(CS2)). Note: (d) concerns the **original** $F$ (subtraction terms built from unrewritten $F$, evaluated block-wise by (e)); for collinear PDFs the direction is irrelevant, so (e) matters only for TMD. Used by `prop:appN-dy`.
- **命题 `prop:appO-tmd` — TMD 版本** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appO-lcopt}、\cref{asm:appO-envelope}、\cref{asm:appO-mixing}、\cref{asm:appO-deformation}、\cref{asm:appO-rewritten-cs}、\cref{asm:appN-envelope}、\cref{asm:appN-separation}、\cref{asm:appN-ward-product}（及 \cref{prop:appO-past-wilson}）。（本命题只涉及部分子态。）Measurement $\delta^{(2)}(\mathbf q_T-\mathbf q_{T,\ell\ell})$, TMD modes and approximators (`def:appN-tmd-approx`), $\lambda=q_T/Q$. Then (i), (ii), (iv), (v) of `thm:appO-main` hold (soft-scale errors relative $O(\lambda)$, `lem:appO-glauber-pc`(e)), and (iii) is replaced by: (iii′) a deformation target moving no transverse component exists, on which the active-line soft approximation holds with relative error $O(\lambda^a)$; after summing graphs and attachments the Ward identities give the unsubtracted TMD correlators $\tilde f^{\rm unsub}_A(x_A,\mathbf b_T)$, $\tilde f^{\rm unsub}_B(x_B,\mathbf b_T)$ (`def:tmd-unsub`) with **past-pointing staple** Wilson lines in $b$ space and a soft factor $\tilde S(\mathbf b_T)$ of past-pointing lines (`def:tmd-soft`), rapidity-regularized as in `def:appN-tmd-approx`. The key identity: in $b$ space the measurement enters only through the momentum through $V_H$, $V_H^\dagger$; $\sum_X|X\rangle e^{i\mathbf b_T\cdot\mathbf P_{XT}}\langle X|=e^{i\mathbf b_T\cdot\hat{\mathbf P}_T}$, and $[\hat P^-,\hat{\mathbf P}_T]=0$ gives $U(\infty,0)^\dagger e^{i\mathbf b_T\cdot\hat{\mathbf P}_T}U(\infty,0)=e^{i\mathbf b_T\cdot\hat{\mathbf P}_T}$; the localized SS insertion is translation invariant and commutes with $\hat{\mathbf P}_T$, so `lem:appO-unitarity` and `prop:appO-ss-cancel` survive. `\uses{thm:appO-main, asm:appO-lcopt, asm:appO-envelope, asm:appO-mixing, asm:appO-deformation, asm:appO-rewritten-cs, asm:appN-envelope, asm:appN-separation, asm:appN-ward-product, prop:appO-past-wilson, def:appN-tmd-approx, def:tmd-unsub, def:tmd-soft, lem:appO-unitarity, prop:appO-ss-cancel}`. Proof: full given `thm:appO-main` (the deformation has $\mathbf e_{G,T}=0$; the Glauber scale is still $(\lambda^2,\lambda^2,\lambda)$; transverse links do not contribute in covariant Feynman-gauge graphs). Precise form of `lem:tmd-glauber` (Ch14); consistent with `Collins2002` (past-pointing for DY). Input of `prop:appN-tmd-window`.
- **注 `rem:appO-measurement` — 测量相容性的统一判据** (no tag). The pairing of §O.4 and the family cancellation of §O.5 use only that the measurement operator commutes with $U(\infty,0)$ and with the $\tau=0$ localized insertions — the substance of "compatible with the measurement"; $\Pi=1$ and $\Pi=e^{i\mathbf b\cdot\hat{\mathbf P}_T}$ commute, $\Pi_{h_1}$ and $\Pi_{\rm gap}$ do not (pointer `prop:break-measurement`, Ch18, which uses `lem:appO-unitarity`).

### O.10 `sec:appO-checklist` 严格性清单 (1.5 pp)

- **表 `tab:appO-checklist` — 附录 O 的严格性清单** (owner App O). Columns: 步骤 (O-1…O-8) | 陈述 (label) | 地位（完整证明／标度陈述／在假设下） | 误差类型 | 所用假设 | Glauber 特有（是／否） | 文献. Rows: regions and power counting (`lem:appO-glauber-pc`: E-S/E-P; integral form `asm:appN-envelope` — no); LCOPT (`lem:appO-lcopt`, `lem:appO-numerators`: complete; `asm:appO-lcopt`: assumption — no); hard locality and orientation (`lem:appO-hard-locality`, `lem:dy-poles-orientation`: complete under `asm:appO-lcopt` — no); cut sum (`lem:appO-cut-position`, `lem:appO-unitarity`: complete; Hermiticity proved in App J — no); equal-time localization (`lem:appO-equal-time`, `lem:appO-insertion-values`: complete — yes); joint localization (`lem:appO-joint-localization`: complete — sector rule, error bound with $\sum_\sigma|w_\sigma|$, closed-quadrant lemma, continuity with a kink — yes); multi-pole and pre-integration localization (`lem:appO-multipole-localization`, `lem:appO-preintegration-localization`: complete — yes); SS localization and family cancellation (`prop:appO-ss-localize`, `prop:appO-ss-cancel`: complete on window cores; window-edge terms vanish with side-independent windows, `lem:appO-side-independent-windows` — yes); coincident $O(g^4)$ patterns in $\mathbb M_{\rm glob}$ (`prop:appO-twoexch-spectator`, `prop:appO-twoexch-regulated`, `prop:appO-halfcoinc-sensitivity`, `prop:appO-pergraph-weights`: model proofs complete, [P] as QCD statements; `prop:appO-strict-window-shape`: R_PT; cut direct lines open, `rem:appO-cut-ss` — yes); $O(g^6)$ direct-line class (`prop:appO-g6-ladder`: [A] — yes); Glauber operator (`lem:appO-glauber-operator`, `prop:dy-eikonal-twogluon`: complete — yes); one-sided rewriting (`prop:appO-one-sided`: under `asm:appO-envelope` (yes), `asm:appO-mixing`(a′)(b) for general $S$ (yes)); deformation (`lem:appO-no-crossing`, `prop:appO-target`: complete for $\mathcal G_0$/graded; `asm:appO-deformation`(a)–(d) — yes); eikonal (`prop:appO-eikonal`: complete, E-P — yes); Wilson lines (`prop:appO-past-wilson`: [A], `asm:appO-rewritten-cs` — yes); soft factor (`lem:method-soft-unitarity`: complete — no); assembly (`thm:appO-main`, `cor:appO-interface`, `prop:appO-tmd`: complete under the listed assumptions; E-W). **Corrected bookkeeping (ERR-M29):** the Glauber-specific unproven inputs are `asm:appO-mixing`(c), `asm:appO-deformation`(a)–(d), `asm:appO-mixing`(a′)(b) for general soft subgraphs, `asm:appO-envelope` **and** `asm:appO-rewritten-cs`; the App N inputs `asm:appN-envelope`, `asm:appN-separation`, `asm:appN-ward-product` are shared with DIS and $e^+e^-$; the entries `asm:appO-adjacent-swap`, `asm:appO-real-jets` (\tagP) and `asm:appO-collinear-ends` (\tagA) enter only the QCD transfers and physical readings of the model theorems, not `thm:appO-main`.

### O.11 `sec:appO-neighbours` 相邻问题 (1 pp; cut first if pages are short)

- **注 `rem:appO-hh` — $pp\to h_1h_2X$ 在 (O-3) 失效** (no tag). Observed jets are not merged into $H$; pairing needs completeness ($U^\dagger\Pi_{h_1}U\ne\Pi_{h_1}$); the equal-time rewriting needs $k^-$-independent external factors — the relevant pinch region is the Glauber region **relative to the pair of directions** $(n_A,w_1)$ ($k\cdot n_A\sim k\cdot w_1\sim\lambda^2$, $\mathbf k_T$ nearly orthogonal to $\mathbf w_{1T}$), where $J_1$ sees $w_1\cdot k\sim\lambda^2$ and depends on $k^-$ at the jet scale (ERR-M31); the Glauber colour operator with opposite signs for initial and final partons is $\mathbf T_a+2\mathbf T_b$ in all-outgoing convention for the configuration of `prop:break-colour` (VF-REF-16; ERR-S2; pointer `CollinsQiu2007`, `RogersMulders2010`, Ch18).
- **注 `rem:appO-gaps` — 快度间隙与衍射** (no tag). A gap projector commutes neither with the evolution nor with the $\tau=0$ insertions: $\langle\cdots\mathcal W'^\dagger\Pi_{\rm gap}\mathcal W\cdots\rangle\ne\langle\cdots\Pi_{\rm gap}\cdots\rangle$ (gap survival); diffractive DIS is safe (one hadron; (O-3) not needed). Two-Glauber factorization violation for single-scale observables: contested — `Gaunt2014` vs `Schwartz:2018obd` (pure Glauber ladders cancel; the leading violation needs a soft gluon via the Lipatov vertex, i.e. Glauber–soft mixing); explicit counterexample for MPI-sensitive observables `Zeng:2015iba`. Pointer `prop:break-hh-diffraction` (Ch18).
- **注 `rem:appO-dft` — Doria–Frenkel–Taylor** (no tag). Outside the premises (coloured massive incoming partons; no uniform-in-$\varepsilon$ envelope); heuristically located in the dropped $\frac{m^2}{2(p_A^+)^2}k^+$ term of the incoming denominator, which couples the two jets' orderings (VF-REF-8); power suppressed; pointer `rem:dy-dft-premises` (Ch12) with `DoriaFrenkelTaylor1980`, `DiLietoEtAl1981`, `Frenkel:1983di`, `Catani:1985xt`, `Andrasi:1980qw`, and `Basu:1984ba` (factorization of power corrections fails at $m^4/s^2$).
- **注 `rem:appO-double-dy` — 双 Drell–Yan** (no tag). (O-2) generalizes (both hard vertices at $\tau=0$; DPD fields at $x^+=0$); (O-3) seems to generalize but is not written; new inputs (colour-correlated DPDs; $1\to2$ splitting) lie in (O-7) and in region overlaps (`Diehl:2015bca`, `Diehl:2017kgu`); pointer `thm:break-double-dy` (Ch18).
- **注 `rem:appO-rs-dictionary` — 与 Glauber SCET 的对照** (no tag). Instantaneous potential ↔ equal-time insertion ($V_G$, $\chi$); Glauber collapse ↔ contact term $c[O(0),V_H(0)]$; causality cancellation ↔ largest-time pairing + family sum; soft–Glauber overlap ↔ App N zero-bin; Glauber–soft mixing ↔ `asm:appO-mixing`; the RS regulator $|2k^z|^{-\eta}$ realizes the common ordering ($p_{\rm opp}=0$) (`RothsteinStewart2016`); pointer `rem:scet-glauber-dictionary` (Ch15).
- **注 `rem:appO-literature-comparison` — 与其他全阶论证的比较；TMD 版本；有争议的提议** (no tag). (1) *Graph-by-graph argument* (`Diehl:2015bca` §4, single DY): region approximants with subtraction terms integrated over all loop momenta (soft–collinear overlaps removed by the subtraction terms of larger regions); cut sums in LCPT; the final-state factor of $A$, summed over cuts and integrated over $k^-$, equals 0 or 1, so the final-state pinches disappear (with a routing assumption the authors state as "desirable to have a formal proof"); Grammer–Yennie and Ward identities give Wilson lines. Each forward graph has a fixed colour factor and insertions are never localized at equal times in both jets, so **no ordering weights arise**: no gap of that argument is identified here, and every "needed / not needed" statement about `asm:appO-mixing`(c) in this appendix is relative to App O's two-time framework. (2) `AybatSterman2009`: an outline in hybrid LCOPT form; spectator attachments cancel by their causal identity (the sector-sum identity of `lem:appO-joint-localization`(c)). (3) `Zhou:2017his` (SCET with explicit Glauber fields, the strict Glauber propagator and the Rothstein–Stewart regulator): the spectator–spectator sector of one light-cone component is generated by one unitary operator along a common time-like direction — in App O's language the common-ordering prescription ($p_{\rm opp}=0$, real half-coincident $W_{12}$) —, the other sector along a second direction; their caveat that the graphical discussion is not a strict proof qualifies their graphical section; our non-acceptance of it as a proof of `asm:appO-collinear-ends` is our own assessment of its key step (one evolution parameter for both jets), and we read it as an EFT form of that matching statement; a related SCET treatment: `Liu:2008cc`. (4) *TMD version:* the localization lemmas hold verbatim at fixed transverse momenta, but the coincident-pattern results are [C] for TMD (regulators vs the TMD rapidity regulator and Collins–Soper evolution, soft-scale windows, staple direction: ${\rm Im}\,p_{\rm opp}$ and ${\rm Im}W_{12}$ are exactly the Coulomb/Glauber phases that distinguish past- from future-pointing staples, `Collins2002`, `CollinsMetz2004`); `Boer:2017hqr` compute the double-Boer–Mulders Drell–Yan term at $O(\alpha_s^2)$ in a quark–scalar-diquark model and find standard factorization once the three-gluon-vertex graph is included (spectator–active patterns and imaginary parts — neither confirming nor contradicting the results here). (5) *Dissenting proposal:* `Chang:2013pba`, `Li:2022fvk` attribute the Lam–Tung violation in pion-induced DY to a non-cancelling Glauber phase in $k_T$ factorization (a single fitted constant); recorded as an unconfirmed phenomenological proposal in tension with [A]-level factorization for inclusive DY (the non-cancellation is asserted by reference to processes with observed final hadrons, `Collins2007`, `CollinsQiu2007`); the statements of this appendix are perturbative and model-level and cannot exclude a non-perturbative pion-specific effect. (6) Recent: collinear factorization violation and reggeization in space-like limits, `Barcaro:2026dsd` (preprint).

# PART 1c — Global rules for the appendices M–Q (verbatim)

## Global rules for the appendices M–Q (the former appendix-draft §00; binding for App M–Q)

### 00.1 Units, files, budgets (lead-author decision)

| unit | key | file | 中文标题 (binding) | pp | writer |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| M | `appM` | `appM_ope.tex` | OPE：微扰证明、无质量 QCD、非微扰条件定理 | 24 | one writer (recommended: the Ch7 `dis` writer, after Ch7 statements are frozen) |
| N | `appN` | `appN_subtraction.tex` | Collins 减除法的精确形式 | 24 | one writer (recommended: also writes Ch9 `sec:method-subtraction`) — written **first** among M/N/O |
| O | `appO` | `appO_glauber.tex` | 光锥排序微扰论与 Drell–Yan 的 Glauber 相消 | 32 | one writer after the App N labels of §N.3–§N.5 are frozen (recommended: also writes Ch12 `sec:dy-glauber`, `sec:dy-model`, `sec:dy-theorem`) |
| P | `appP` | `appP_blueprint.tex` | 形式化蓝图：假设登记表、依赖图与状态表——由 \uses 数据自动生成 + 导言 | 10 | introduction by a writer (≈2 pp, last); tables and figures generated by the integrator |
| Q | `appQ` | `appQ_errata.tex` | 经核实的文献勘误 | 4 | written after `verify/` completes |

Labels: appendix `\label{app:appM}` … `\label{app:appQ}`; sections `sec:appM-…`; statements `def|asm|lem|prop|thm|cor|ex|rem:appM-…`; tables `tab:appM-…`; figures as in FIGURES_INDEX_v2. Chapter-owned statements whose proofs are delivered here keep their chapter labels (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §5.3, §6.3); they are listed in the **proof-delivery table** of each appendix and are **not** in the registry of the appendix.

### 00.2 How each entry is written (in addition to WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4, §6)

1. **Entry format.** `**类型 `label` — 中文标题** [tag, qualifier]` · premise sentence (R_cond) · partonic/non-perturbative line · *Statement* (formulas in the binding conventions) · `\uses{…}` · *Proof plan* (full / sketch; numbered steps; literature with location; checks with script paths) · *VF* IDs · *Error type* of the conclusion (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.7) · *Surpass/correction* note (ERR IDs refer to `refdoc/REFDOC_ANALYSIS.md` §2; never printed).
2. **Partonic line.** Every App N and App O statement concerns partonic integrands order by order; its hypotheses end with 「（本命题只涉及部分子态。）」. App M §M.2–§M.7 likewise (massive models, partonic Green functions). App M §M.8 is hadronic: its R_cond statements list `asm:intro-*` in the premise sentence and end with 「非微扰地位：见前提（MA0、MA3、MA4）。」.
3. **Qualifier sentences** are the verbatim strings of WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.3; the R_cond premise sentence 「前提：\cref{asm:…}、…」 lists every assumption and every non-[R] statement used, and the same labels are in `\uses`.
4. **`\uses` order inside one appendix file.** A label may be used only after it is defined in the same file (check C4). The order of the entries below respects this; writers keep it. Appendix statements may use statements of any chapter and of other appendices (no cycles; App P checks).
5. **Chapter statements proved here.** The appendix section starts with 「\cref{…} 的证明」; the statement is not repeated as an environment. The restatement given in this outline (marked *Target*) is for the appendix writer only; if it differs from the chapter's wording in `FINAL_OUTLINE_v2`, the chapter wording governs and the writer reports the difference. The chapter statement's `\uses` must contain the appendix lemmas its proof uses; the column "appendix lemmas for the chapter `\uses`" of the proof-delivery tables gives them (for the merge into the core outline).
6. **The reference document** is never cited, quoted or named (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.10). Internal identifiers such as "Thm D.10", "Ass. E.33" appear in this outline only for traceability. Every adopted construction is re-derived and attributed to the primary literature (Zimmermann; Lowenstein–Zimmermann; Joglekar–Lee; Weinberg; Collins 2011; Collins–Soper–Sterman; Bodwin; Aybat–Sterman; Diehl–Gaunt–Ostermeier–Plößl–Schäfer; Kogut–Soper; Veltman; Rothstein–Stewart; …) or presented as our derivation.
7. **Error types.** Each status table (`tab:appM-status`, `tab:appN-status`, `tab:appO-checklist`) has the column 「误差类型」 with E-S / E-P / E-F / E-N / E-N$^\varphi$ (fibered integral norm, `def:appN-fibered`; v2.3) / E-W, and each `proofsketch` step that rests on power counting names its type.
8. **Box** 「本附录证明了什么／没有证明什么」 right after the first paragraph of App M, N, O (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.6 frame; labels only). Drafts are given below.
9. **Conventions.** Light-cone vectors $n_A=(1^+,0^-,\mathbf 0_T)$, $n_B=(0^+,1^-,\mathbf 0_T)$ (row 2), never $n,\bar n$ (the reference's $n,\bar n$ are our $n_A,n_B$). Projections $\pi_+(k)=(n_B\cdot k)\,n_A=(k^+,0,\mathbf 0_T)$, $\pi_-(k)=(n_A\cdot k)\,n_B=(0,k^-,\mathbf 0_T)$. Glauber direction $e_G=n_B-n_A$ (row 38, VF-REF-7). Eikonal rules VF-WL-1 (row 28): past-pointing $W[0,-\infty n]$: $\prod_j\frac{i}{n\cdot(k_1+\dots+k_j)+i0}$; future-pointing $W[\infty n,0]$: $\prod_j\frac{-i}{n\cdot(k_j+\dots+k_m)-i0}$. Coupling $D_\mu=\partial_\mu-igA_\mu^at^a$, quark–gluon vertex $+ig\gamma^\mu t^a$ (the reference's "$-igJ^{+a}$" is a sign slip, ERR-m26).

### 00.3 Tag policy for the technical assumptions (consistency with the grade-propagation rule)

The grade-propagation rule (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.6) makes every process theorem carry at most the weakest tag of the technical assumptions it uses. The binding master-table tags of the collinear and TMD process theorems are [A]. Therefore (details and justification per entry in `ASSUMPTIONS.md` §00.3):
- a technical assumption that encodes a step of an **accepted all-order argument** of the literature (CSS, Bodwin, Collins 2011, Sterman) which has not been written in the precise form required here is tagged `\tagA`, and its 「地位」 field says "open in the precise form stated; covered at the level of the standard all-order argument by …" — this applies to all `asm:appM-*`, all `asm:appN-*` except `asm:appN-values` (convention, no tag), `asm:appN-ir-regulator` (`\tagC`) and `asm:appN-brst-nonlocal` (`\tagC`, named gap, merge decision MD1), and all `asm:appO-*` except `asm:appO-adjacent-swap` and `asm:appO-real-jets` (`\tagP`: proven in the scalar spectator model, argued for QCD jets; they enter only the QCD transfer of the App O model theorems, which are [P], and are premises of no [A] statement; v2.3);
- an assumption that is **not** covered by any accepted argument is tagged `\tagC` (or `\tagP` if partly proven) and is never a premise of an [A] statement: `asm:intro-*` (global non-perturbative, excluded from propagation for ordinary statements), `asm:appN-ir-regulator`, the hadronic LaMET hypotheses, the non-perturbative analyticity hypotheses of the Liu programme;
- the register of REFDOC §5.3 is refined accordingly: `(Z2)` is split off as `asm:appN-ir-regulator`; the non-local BRST classification assumed by Collins 2011 §11.3.2 becomes the explicit entry `asm:appN-brst-nonlocal` — `\tagC` at the merge (MD1: no all-order argument; frontier/others §8.3 assesses the general multilocal case as [C]); a premise only of `thm:appN-structure` and a named gap of the chapter [A] statements.

### 00.4 Outcomes of the proof extensions X1, X2, X3 (verify patch v2.3)

The verification workflow (`verify/`) ran three proof extensions: **X1 Glauber** (App O), **X2 subtraction** (App N), **X3 OPE** (App M); final reports `verify/extend_reports/X1-glauber_final.md`, `X2-subtraction_final.md`, `X3-ope_final.md` (each with an independent second review). Decision rules (applied):
- **PASS** = two independent confirmations recorded in `verify/` (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.5 item 2) and, for a novelty claim, a literature check: the statement is inserted with its tag, the dependent status lines, the assumption register and `tab:appX-status`/App P are updated.
- **PARTIAL** = the extension holds in a restricted setting: the statement is inserted with the restriction in its first sentence and tag [P] (or [R, R_PT] for an explicit fixed-order statement whose first sentence states the order; a model theorem is [P] as a QCD statement, WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.3 item 4).
- **FAIL / not delivered** = the reserved slot label is withdrawn (named below only by its slot ID; §10), the fallback stays, no claim is printed.

| ERR-M29 | App O §O.10 | the Glauber-specific open inputs include `asm:appO-envelope` and `asm:appO-rewritten-cs` ((CS1)(CS2) for rewritten and family-summed forms) |
| ERR-M31 | App O §O.11 | $pp\to h_1h_2X$: the Glauber region relative to the pair $(n_A,w_1)$ |
| ERR-M32 | App O §O.1 | method credited to Aybat–Sterman 2009 and Diehl–Gaunt–Ostermeier–Plößl–Schäfer 2016 |
| ERR-m23 | App M §M.4 | `ex:appM-unreduced`: $-\kappa\pi^2/(2q^4)+O(m^2q^{-6}\ln q)$, no logarithm (VF-REF-14) |
| ERR-m24 | App M §M.2–§M.3, §M.8 | disconnected $\omega$ (`cor:appM-weinberg-degree`); routing for frozen $q$ (`lem:appM-routing`); slice edge-of-the-wedge with $Q^2$-smearing (`lem:appM-positivity`); mass-mixing rescaling (`lem:appM-norm-bound`) |
| ERR-m25 | App N §N.5, §N.7 | the correct $i0$ reason in DIS; $C_S$'s $k^+$ integral is a Beta function; the exact NLP replaces the empirical fit; Table-5-type comparisons must use $\tilde Q^2$; (Abs) edge case (`rem:appN-abs-edge`) |
| ERR-m26 | App O §O.5 | corrected proof of the full-axis localization (close in the LHP); window-shape statement needs $K_{1,2}\le\Lambda/2$, error $O(\lambda_J/\min K)$; vertex $+ig$; absolute (not relative) bound in the SS localization |
| ERR-m27 | App O §O.6–§O.8 | compensating term $+\frac14C_A\chi^2X$; $\sigma$ restored in ${\rm Im}(n\cdot K)=t\sigma\sum f_a$; SA deformation statement at $0<\Theta'_j<1$; boundary-layer step checked against `def:method-neighbourhoods`; Wilson-line prescription "proved up to `asm:appO-rewritten-cs`" |

A slip in our own analysis is corrected here as well: REFDOC S48 states that in `thm:appO-main` "every soft attachment to $J_A$ ($J_B$) is replaced by a past-pointing Wilson line along $n_B$ ($n_A$)". Correct (eikonal factor $n_A^\mu/(n_A\cdot k)$ for a soft gluon on an $A$-collinear line): soft attachments to $J_A$ ($J_B$) give past-pointing lines along $n_A$ ($n_B$) **in the soft factor**, and $A$-collinear longitudinal gluons attached to $H$ or $J_B$ give the past-pointing line along $n_B$ **in the collinear factor of $A$** (c_glauber §1.5 Steps 1–2). The statements below use the correct form.
